    41	    EX_RUNDOWN_REF PagingRundown;
    42	    BOOLEAN ReadOnly;
    43	    BOOLEAN Sealed;
    44	    NTSTATUS DrainStatus;
    45	    UNICODE_STRING StageName;
    46	    WCHAR StageBuffer[SAFEUPLOAD_MAX_PATH_CHARS];
    47	    PSAFEUPLOAD_EXCHANGE RenameExchange;
    48	    PFLT_INSTANCE OriginalInstance;
    49	    PFLT_INSTANCE BackingInstance;
    50	    HANDLE BackingHandle;
    51	    PFILE_OBJECT BackingObject;
    52	    ULONG SectorSize;
    53	    BOOLEAN ResourceInitialized;
    54	    BOOLEAN PagingInitialized;
    55	    USHORT VolumeLength;
    56	    UNICODE_STRING Name;
    57	    WCHAR NameBuffer[SAFEUPLOAD_MAX_PATH_CHARS];
    58	} STAGE_STREAM, *PSTAGE_STREAM;
    59	
    60	typedef struct _STAGE_HANDLE {
    61	    PSTAGE_STREAM Stream;
    62	    ACCESS_MASK GrantedAccess;
    63	    BOOLEAN Cleaned;
    64	    ULONG LockOwnerCount;
    65	    PEPROCESS LockOwners[16]; /* Duplicate handles can issue locks in other processes. */
    66	} STAGE_HANDLE, *PSTAGE_HANDLE;

    68	static LIST_ENTRY StageStreams;
    69	static KSPIN_LOCK StageListLock;
    70	static ERESOURCE StageNamespaceResource;
    71	static volatile ULONG StageStreamCount;
    72	static volatile LONG64 StageLastUnloadVeto;
    73	static LONG64 StageNamespaceSequence;
    74	static volatile LONG StageFileObjects;
    75	static BOOLEAN StageStopping;
    76	static LIST_ENTRY StageViews;
    77	static KEVENT StageWorkerStop;
    78	static HANDLE StageWorkerHandle;

    81	/* Native spin-lock transitions are isolated from callers in resident SAL helpers. */
    82	_IRQL_requires_max_(DISPATCH_LEVEL)
    83	_IRQL_raises_(DISPATCH_LEVEL)
    84	__declspec(noinline) static VOID StageStreamAcquireSpinLock(
    85	    _In_ PKSPIN_LOCK Lock, _Out_ _At_(*OldIrql, _IRQL_saves_) PKIRQL OldIrql)
    86	{
    87	    KeAcquireSpinLock(Lock, OldIrql);
    88	}
    89	
    90	_IRQL_requires_(DISPATCH_LEVEL)
    91	__declspec(noinline) static VOID StageStreamReleaseSpinLock(
    92	    _In_ PKSPIN_LOCK Lock, _In_ _IRQL_restores_ KIRQL OldIrql)
    93	{
    94	    KeReleaseSpinLock(Lock, OldIrql);
    95	}
    96	
    97	#if SAFEUPLOAD_STAGING_PROTOTYPE
    98	// Static storage is nonpaged and gives the callback path a bounded ring with
    99	// no allocation or lifetime race. An odd control state means tracing is on.
   100	volatile LONG SafeUploadAdmissionTraceControlState;
   101	volatile LONG SafeUploadAdmissionTraceSectionEvents;
   102	static volatile LONG AdmissionTraceLifetimeEvents;
   103	#define STAGE_TRACE_WATCH_SLOTS 64
   104	// Section object pointers of streams that got a writable CreateSection while the lifetime option was on.
   105	// Observe-only: it only widens which cleanup/close events are recorded. A full table drops new entries.
   106	static volatile PVOID AdmissionTraceWatch[STAGE_TRACE_WATCH_SLOTS];
   107	DECLSPEC_ALIGN(8) static SAFEUPLOAD_ADMISSION_TRACE_ENTRY AdmissionTraceRing[
   108	    SAFEUPLOAD_ADMISSION_TRACE_RING_ENTRIES];
   109	DECLSPEC_ALIGN(8) static volatile LONG64 AdmissionTraceNextSequence;
   110	static volatile LONG AdmissionTraceWriterBusy;
   111	static SAFEUPLOAD_ADMISSION_TRACE_COUNTERS AdmissionTraceCounters;
   112	static EX_RUNDOWN_REF AdmissionTraceRundown;
   113	static FAST_MUTEX AdmissionTraceControlMutex;
   114	static BOOLEAN AdmissionTraceRundownClosed;
   115	
   116	static LONG StageAdmissionTraceNextState(_In_ LONG CurrentState, _In_ BOOLEAN Enable)
   117	{
   118	    ULONG nextState = (((ULONG)CurrentState & 0x7ffffffeUL) + 2UL) & 0x7ffffffeUL;
   119	    if (Enable) nextState |= 1UL;
   120	    return (LONG)nextState;
   121	}
   122	
   123	static VOID StageAdmissionTraceReset(VOID)
   124	{
   125	    ULONG index;
   126	    InterlockedExchange64(&AdmissionTraceNextSequence, 0);
   127	    for (index = 0; index < SAFEUPLOAD_ADMISSION_TRACE_RING_ENTRIES; index += 1) {
   128	        InterlockedExchange64((volatile LONG64 *)&AdmissionTraceRing[index].Sequence, 0);
   129	    }
   130	    InterlockedExchange(&AdmissionTraceWriterBusy, 0);
   131	    RtlZeroMemory(&AdmissionTraceCounters, sizeof(AdmissionTraceCounters));
   132	    for (index = 0; index < STAGE_TRACE_WATCH_SLOTS; index += 1) {
   133	        InterlockedExchangePointer((PVOID volatile *)&AdmissionTraceWatch[index], NULL);
   134	    }
   135	}
   136	
   137	static VOID StageAdmissionTraceSnapshotCounters(
   138	    _Out_ PSAFEUPLOAD_ADMISSION_TRACE_COUNTERS Destination)
   139	{
   140	    Destination->TotalEvents = (UINT64)InterlockedCompareExchange64(
   141	        (volatile LONG64 *)&AdmissionTraceCounters.TotalEvents, 0, 0);
   142	    Destination->PagingWrites = (UINT64)InterlockedCompareExchange64(
   143	        (volatile LONG64 *)&AdmissionTraceCounters.PagingWrites, 0, 0);
   144	    Destination->NonPagingWrites = (UINT64)InterlockedCompareExchange64(
   145	        (volatile LONG64 *)&AdmissionTraceCounters.NonPagingWrites, 0, 0);
   146	    Destination->SectionAcquires = (UINT64)InterlockedCompareExchange64(
   147	        (volatile LONG64 *)&AdmissionTraceCounters.SectionAcquires, 0, 0);
   148	    Destination->SectionReleases = (UINT64)InterlockedCompareExchange64(
   149	        (volatile LONG64 *)&AdmissionTraceCounters.SectionReleases, 0, 0);
   150	    Destination->InstanceSetups = (UINT64)InterlockedCompareExchange64(
   151	        (volatile LONG64 *)&AdmissionTraceCounters.InstanceSetups, 0, 0);
   152	    Destination->LostEntries = (UINT64)InterlockedCompareExchange64(
   153	        (volatile LONG64 *)&AdmissionTraceCounters.LostEntries, 0, 0);
   154	}
   155	
   156	BOOLEAN SafeUploadStageAdmissionTraceBegin(_In_ LONG TraceState)
   157	{
   158	    if ((TraceState & 1) == 0 || !ExAcquireRundownProtection(&AdmissionTraceRundown)) {
   159	        return FALSE;
   160	    }
   161	
   162	    KeMemoryBarrier();
   163	    if (SafeUploadAdmissionTraceControlState != TraceState) {
   164	        ExReleaseRundownProtection(&AdmissionTraceRundown);
   165	        return FALSE;
   166	    }
   167	
   168	    return TRUE;
   169	}
   170	
   171	VOID SafeUploadStageAdmissionTraceEnd(VOID)
   172	{
   173	    ExReleaseRundownProtection(&AdmissionTraceRundown);
   174	}
   175	
   176	static BOOLEAN StageAdmissionTraceRecordInternal(
   177	    _In_ const SAFEUPLOAD_ADMISSION_TRACE_ENTRY *Entry,
   178	    _In_ BOOLEAN WaitForWriter)
   179	{
   180	    SAFEUPLOAD_ADMISSION_TRACE_ENTRY copy;
   181	    PSAFEUPLOAD_ADMISSION_TRACE_ENTRY slot;
   182	    LARGE_INTEGER timestamp;
   183	    LONGLONG signedSequence;
   184	    LONGLONG lastCommittedSequence;
   185	    UINT64 sequence;
   186	    ULONG index;
   187	
   188	    InterlockedIncrement64((volatile LONG64 *)&AdmissionTraceCounters.TotalEvents);
   189	    switch (Entry->EventKind) {
   190	    case SAFEUPLOAD_ADMISSION_TRACE_EVENT_PAGING_WRITE:
   191	        InterlockedIncrement64((volatile LONG64 *)&AdmissionTraceCounters.PagingWrites);
   192	        break;
   193	    case SAFEUPLOAD_ADMISSION_TRACE_EVENT_UNOWNED_NONPAGING_WRITE:
   194	        InterlockedIncrement64((volatile LONG64 *)&AdmissionTraceCounters.NonPagingWrites);
   195	        break;
   196	    case SAFEUPLOAD_ADMISSION_TRACE_EVENT_SECTION_ACQUIRE:
   197	        InterlockedIncrement64((volatile LONG64 *)&AdmissionTraceCounters.SectionAcquires);
   198	        break;
   199	    case SAFEUPLOAD_ADMISSION_TRACE_EVENT_SECTION_RELEASE:
   200	        InterlockedIncrement64((volatile LONG64 *)&AdmissionTraceCounters.SectionReleases);
   201	        break;
   202	    case SAFEUPLOAD_ADMISSION_TRACE_EVENT_INSTANCE_SETUP:
   203	        InterlockedIncrement64((volatile LONG64 *)&AdmissionTraceCounters.InstanceSetups);
   204	        break;
   205	    default:
   206	        break;
   207	    }
   208	
   209	    // Callback writers drop on reservation contention. The explicit probe
   210	    // may wait here while appending its one user-requested entry.
   211	    while (InterlockedCompareExchange(&AdmissionTraceWriterBusy, 1, 0) != 0) {
   212	        if (!WaitForWriter) {
   213	            InterlockedIncrement64((volatile LONG64 *)&AdmissionTraceCounters.LostEntries);
   214	            return FALSE;
   215	        }
   216	        YieldProcessor();
   217	    }
   218	
   219	    lastCommittedSequence = InterlockedCompareExchange64(&AdmissionTraceNextSequence, 0, 0);
   220	    signedSequence = lastCommittedSequence + 1;
   221	    sequence = (UINT64)signedSequence;
   222	    index = (ULONG)((sequence - 1ULL) &
   223	        ((UINT64)SAFEUPLOAD_ADMISSION_TRACE_RING_ENTRIES - 1ULL));
   224	    slot = &AdmissionTraceRing[index];
   225	
   226	    if (sequence > SAFEUPLOAD_ADMISSION_TRACE_RING_ENTRIES) {
   227	        InterlockedIncrement64((volatile LONG64 *)&AdmissionTraceCounters.LostEntries);
   228	    }
   229	
   230	    // Zero marks an in-progress overwrite. The reader-visible high-water mark
   231	    // is updated only after the slot sequence is committed below.
   232	    InterlockedExchange64((volatile LONG64 *)&slot->Sequence, 0);
   233	    KeQuerySystemTime(&timestamp);
   234	    copy = *Entry;
   235	    copy.Sequence = sequence;
   236	    copy.Timestamp = (UINT64)timestamp.QuadPart;
   237	    RtlCopyMemory(&slot->Timestamp,
   238	                  &copy.Timestamp,
   239	                  sizeof(copy) - FIELD_OFFSET(SAFEUPLOAD_ADMISSION_TRACE_ENTRY, Timestamp));
   240	    KeMemoryBarrier();
   241	    InterlockedExchange64((volatile LONG64 *)&slot->Sequence, (LONG64)sequence);
   242	    KeMemoryBarrier();
   243	    InterlockedExchange64(&AdmissionTraceNextSequence, signedSequence);
   244	    InterlockedExchange(&AdmissionTraceWriterBusy, 0);
   245	    return TRUE;
   246	}
   247	
   248	VOID SafeUploadStageAdmissionTraceRecord(
   249	    _In_ const SAFEUPLOAD_ADMISSION_TRACE_ENTRY *Entry)
   250	{
   251	    (VOID)StageAdmissionTraceRecordInternal(Entry, FALSE);
   252	}
   253	
   254	VOID SafeUploadStageAdmissionTraceNoteLost(_In_ LONG TraceState)
   255	{
   256	    if (SafeUploadStageAdmissionTraceBegin(TraceState)) {
   257	        InterlockedIncrement64((volatile LONG64 *)&AdmissionTraceCounters.LostEntries);
   258	        SafeUploadStageAdmissionTraceEnd();
   259	    }
   260	}
   261	
   262	VOID SafeUploadStageAdmissionTraceNoteTicketLost(VOID)
   263	{
   264	    /* Caller holds recorder rundown or an outstanding ticket, so CLEAR cannot
   265	     * pass its off-state/drain check before this explicit loss is visible. */
   266	    InterlockedIncrement64((volatile LONG64 *)&AdmissionTraceCounters.LostEntries);
   267	}
   268	
   269	NTSTATUS SafeUploadStageAdmissionTraceControl(_In_ UINT32 Command, _In_ UINT32 Options)
   270	{
   271	    LONG state;
   272	    LONG64 lostBeforeClose;
   273	    BOOLEAN wasEnabled;
   274	    NTSTATUS status = STATUS_SUCCESS;
   275	
   276	    PAGED_CODE();
   277	    ExAcquireFastMutex(&AdmissionTraceControlMutex);
   278	    state = InterlockedCompareExchange(&SafeUploadAdmissionTraceControlState, 0, 0);
   279	    wasEnabled = (state & 1) != 0;
   280	
   281	    if (Command == SAFEUPLOAD_CONTROL_ADMISSION_TRACE_ENABLE) {
   282	        InterlockedExchange(&SafeUploadAdmissionTraceSectionEvents,
   283	                            (Options & SAFEUPLOAD_ADMISSION_TRACE_OPTION_SECTION_EVENTS) != 0 ? 1 : 0);
   284	        InterlockedExchange(&AdmissionTraceLifetimeEvents,
   285	                            (Options & SAFEUPLOAD_ADMISSION_TRACE_OPTION_FILE_LIFETIME) != 0 ? 1 : 0);
   286	        if (!wasEnabled) {
   287	            if (AdmissionTraceRundownClosed) {
   288	                ExReInitializeRundownProtection(&AdmissionTraceRundown);
   289	                AdmissionTraceRundownClosed = FALSE;
   290	            }
   291	            InterlockedExchange(&SafeUploadAdmissionTraceControlState,
   292	                                StageAdmissionTraceNextState(state, TRUE));
   293	        }
   294	    } else if (Command == SAFEUPLOAD_CONTROL_ADMISSION_TRACE_DISABLE ||
   295	               Command == SAFEUPLOAD_CONTROL_ADMISSION_TRACE_CLEAR) {
   296	        lostBeforeClose = InterlockedCompareExchange64(
   297	            (volatile LONG64 *)&AdmissionTraceCounters.LostEntries, 0, 0);
   298	        InterlockedExchange(&SafeUploadAdmissionTraceControlState,
   299	                            StageAdmissionTraceNextState(state, FALSE));
   300	        if (!AdmissionTraceRundownClosed) {
   301	            ExWaitForRundownProtectionRelease(&AdmissionTraceRundown);
   302	            AdmissionTraceRundownClosed = TRUE;
   303	        }
   304	
   305	        /* The off-state plus drained recorder rundown closes admission for new
   306	         * observer tickets. Refuse disable/clear while an already paired W
   307	         * ticket is live, then reopen recording so its matching end is retained. */
   308	        if (SafeUploadStageWritersObserverTicketsOutstanding() != 0 ||
   309	            InterlockedCompareExchange64((volatile LONG64 *)&AdmissionTraceCounters.LostEntries, 0, 0) !=
   310	                lostBeforeClose) {
   311	            /* A W_END may retire during the off-state window and record an
   312	             * explicit loss before dropping its outstanding count. Do not let
   313	             * CLEAR erase that loss and turn an unmatched pair into a clean run. */
   314	            if (wasEnabled) {
   315	                ExReInitializeRundownProtection(&AdmissionTraceRundown);
   316	                AdmissionTraceRundownClosed = FALSE;
   317	                state = InterlockedCompareExchange(&SafeUploadAdmissionTraceControlState, 0, 0);
   318	                InterlockedExchange(&SafeUploadAdmissionTraceControlState,
   319	                                    StageAdmissionTraceNextState(state, TRUE));
   320	            }
   321	            status = STATUS_DEVICE_BUSY;
   322	        } else if (Command == SAFEUPLOAD_CONTROL_ADMISSION_TRACE_CLEAR) {
   323	            StageAdmissionTraceReset();
   324	            if (wasEnabled) {
   325	                ExReInitializeRundownProtection(&AdmissionTraceRundown);
   326	                AdmissionTraceRundownClosed = FALSE;
   327	                state = InterlockedCompareExchange(&SafeUploadAdmissionTraceControlState, 0, 0);
   328	                InterlockedExchange(&SafeUploadAdmissionTraceControlState,
   329	                                    StageAdmissionTraceNextState(state, TRUE));
   330	            }
   331	        }
   332	    } else {
   333	        status = STATUS_INVALID_PARAMETER;
   334	    }
   335	
   336	    ExReleaseFastMutex(&AdmissionTraceControlMutex);
   337	    return status;
   338	}
   339	
   340	NTSTATUS SafeUploadStageAdmissionTraceReadBatch(
   341	    _In_ UINT64 Cursor,
   342	    _In_ UINT64 SnapshotSequence,
   343	    _Out_ PSAFEUPLOAD_ADMISSION_TRACE_BATCH Batch)
   344	{
   345	    UINT64 latest;
   346	    UINT64 snapshot;
   347	    UINT64 oldest;
   348	    UINT64 scan;
   349	
   350	    PAGED_CODE();
   351	    if (Batch == NULL) return STATUS_INVALID_PARAMETER;
   352	
   353	    RtlZeroMemory(Batch, sizeof(*Batch));
   354	    ExAcquireFastMutex(&AdmissionTraceControlMutex);
   355	    latest = (UINT64)InterlockedCompareExchange64(&AdmissionTraceNextSequence, 0, 0);
   356	    snapshot = SnapshotSequence == 0 ? latest : SnapshotSequence;
   357	    if (snapshot > latest || (Cursor != 0 && Cursor - 1ULL > snapshot)) {
   358	        ExReleaseFastMutex(&AdmissionTraceControlMutex);
   359	        return STATUS_INVALID_PARAMETER;
   360	    }

 succeeded in 0ms:
   361	
   362	    oldest = latest > SAFEUPLOAD_ADMISSION_TRACE_RING_ENTRIES ?
   363	        latest - SAFEUPLOAD_ADMISSION_TRACE_RING_ENTRIES + 1ULL : 1ULL;
   364	    scan = Cursor == 0 ? oldest : Cursor;
   365	    if (scan < oldest) scan = oldest;
   366	
   367	    Batch->Control.Version = SAFEUPLOAD_PROTOCOL_VERSION;
   368	    Batch->Control.StructSize = sizeof(*Batch);
   369	    Batch->Control.Command = SAFEUPLOAD_CONTROL_ADMISSION_TRACE_READ_BATCH;
   370	    Batch->EntryCount = 0;
   371	    Batch->Cursor = scan;
   372	    Batch->SnapshotSequence = snapshot;
   373	
   374	    while (scan <= snapshot && Batch->EntryCount < SAFEUPLOAD_ADMISSION_TRACE_BATCH_ENTRIES) {
   375	        SAFEUPLOAD_ADMISSION_TRACE_ENTRY copy;
   376	        PSAFEUPLOAD_ADMISSION_TRACE_ENTRY slot;
   377	        UINT64 before;
   378	        UINT64 after;
   379	        ULONG index = (ULONG)((scan - 1ULL) &
   380	            ((UINT64)SAFEUPLOAD_ADMISSION_TRACE_RING_ENTRIES - 1ULL));
   381	
   382	        slot = &AdmissionTraceRing[index];
   383	        before = (UINT64)InterlockedCompareExchange64((volatile LONG64 *)&slot->Sequence, 0, 0);
   384	        if (before == scan) {
   385	            RtlCopyMemory(&copy, slot, sizeof(copy));
   386	            KeMemoryBarrier();
   387	            after = (UINT64)InterlockedCompareExchange64((volatile LONG64 *)&slot->Sequence, 0, 0);
   388	            if (before == after && copy.Sequence == after) {
   389	                Batch->Entries[Batch->EntryCount] = copy;
   390	                Batch->EntryCount += 1;
   391	            } else if (after <= scan || copy.Sequence != before) {
   392	                // If an overwrite has not committed, leave the cursor here
   393	                // so a later read can retry publication.
   394	                break;
   395	            }
   396	        } else if (before < scan) {
   397	            // Sequence tickets are published after their slots commit; this
   398	            // mismatch is a slot transition, so do not skip the cursor.
   399	            break;
   400	        }
   401	
   402	        if (scan == 0xffffffffffffffffULL) break;
   403	        scan += 1ULL;
   404	    }
   405	
   406	    Batch->NextCursor = scan;
   407	    StageAdmissionTraceSnapshotCounters(&Batch->Counters);
   408	    ExReleaseFastMutex(&AdmissionTraceControlMutex);
   409	    return STATUS_SUCCESS;
   410	}
   411	
   412	static VOID StageAdmissionTraceShutdown(VOID)
   413	{
   414	    LONG state;
   415	
   416	    PAGED_CODE();
   417	    ExAcquireFastMutex(&AdmissionTraceControlMutex);
   418	    state = InterlockedCompareExchange(&SafeUploadAdmissionTraceControlState, 0, 0);
   419	    InterlockedExchange(&SafeUploadAdmissionTraceControlState,
   420	                        StageAdmissionTraceNextState(state, FALSE));
   421	    if (!AdmissionTraceRundownClosed) {
   422	        ExWaitForRundownProtectionRelease(&AdmissionTraceRundown);
   423	        AdmissionTraceRundownClosed = TRUE;
   424	    }
   425	    ExReleaseFastMutex(&AdmissionTraceControlMutex);
   426	}
   427	
   428	static VOID StageAdmissionTraceFillOperation(
   429	    _Out_ PSAFEUPLOAD_ADMISSION_TRACE_ENTRY Entry,
   430	    _In_ PFLT_CALLBACK_DATA Data,
   431	    _In_ PCFLT_RELATED_OBJECTS Objects,
   432	    _In_ UINT32 EventKind,
   433	    _In_ BOOLEAN OwnedStream)
   434	{
   435	    PFILE_OBJECT fileObject = Data->Iopb->TargetFileObject;
   436	    KIRQL irql = KeGetCurrentIrql();
   437	
   438	    RtlZeroMemory(Entry, sizeof(*Entry));
   439	    Entry->EventKind = EventKind;
   440	    Entry->Irql = irql;
   441	    Entry->MajorFunction = Data->Iopb->MajorFunction;
   442	    Entry->MinorFunction = Data->Iopb->MinorFunction;
   443	    Entry->IrpFlags = Data->Iopb->IrpFlags;
   444	    Entry->OwnedStream = OwnedStream ? 1u : 0u;
   445	    Entry->AdmissionRecordState = 0;
   446	    Entry->MmDoesResult = SAFEUPLOAD_ADMISSION_TRACE_MMDOES_NOT_APPLICABLE;
   447	    Entry->StreamContextState = SAFEUPLOAD_ADMISSION_TRACE_CONTEXT_NOT_QUERIED;
   448	    Entry->VolumeKind = SafeUploadVolumeUnknown;
   449	    if (KeGetCurrentIrql() <= APC_LEVEL) Entry->ProcessId = FltGetRequestorProcessId(Data);
   450	    if (Objects->Instance != NULL) Entry->Instance = (UINT64)(ULONG_PTR)Objects->Instance;
   451	    if (fileObject != NULL) {
   452	        Entry->TargetFileObject = (UINT64)(ULONG_PTR)fileObject;
   453	        Entry->SectionObjectPointer = (UINT64)(ULONG_PTR)fileObject->SectionObjectPointer;
   454	    } else {
   455	        Entry->StreamContextState = SAFEUPLOAD_ADMISSION_TRACE_CONTEXT_NOT_APPLICABLE;
   456	    }
   457	}
   458	
   459	static BOOLEAN StageTraceWatchContains(_In_opt_ PVOID SectionObjectPointer)
   460	{
   461	    ULONG index;
   462	    if (SectionObjectPointer == NULL) return FALSE;
   463	    for (index = 0; index < STAGE_TRACE_WATCH_SLOTS; index += 1) {
   464	        if (AdmissionTraceWatch[index] == SectionObjectPointer) return TRUE;
   465	    }
   466	    return FALSE;
   467	}
   468	
   469	static VOID StageTraceWatchAdd(_In_opt_ PVOID SectionObjectPointer)
   470	{
   471	    ULONG index;
   472	    if (SectionObjectPointer == NULL || StageTraceWatchContains(SectionObjectPointer)) return;
   473	    for (index = 0; index < STAGE_TRACE_WATCH_SLOTS; index += 1) {
   474	        if (InterlockedCompareExchangePointer((PVOID volatile *)&AdmissionTraceWatch[index],
   475	                                              SectionObjectPointer, NULL) == NULL) return;
   476	    }
   477	}
   478	
   479	// Observe-only. Records IRP_MJ_CLEANUP (last user handle gone) and IRP_MJ_CLOSE (last reference gone)
   480	// of a file object that was opened with write access or whose stream got a writable CreateSection while
   481	// tracing, so the gap between the two, and the lifetime of any internal file object a section keeps, can
   482	// be measured. It runs only when tracing and the file-lifetime option are on, reads no paged data,
   483	// takes no lock beyond the ring's try-lock, and never alters the operation.
   484	static VOID StageTraceFileLifetime(
   485	    _In_ PFLT_CALLBACK_DATA Data,
   486	    _In_ PCFLT_RELATED_OBJECTS Objects,
   487	    _In_ UINT32 EventKind)
   488	{
   489	    LONG traceState = SafeUploadAdmissionTraceControlState;
   490	    PFILE_OBJECT fileObject = Data->Iopb->TargetFileObject;
   491	    SAFEUPLOAD_ADMISSION_TRACE_ENTRY entry;
   492	
   493	    if ((traceState & 1) == 0 || AdmissionTraceLifetimeEvents == 0) return;
   494	    if (fileObject == NULL) return;
   495	    if (!fileObject->WriteAccess && !StageTraceWatchContains(fileObject->SectionObjectPointer)) return;
   496	    if (!SafeUploadStageAdmissionTraceBegin(traceState)) return;
   497	    StageAdmissionTraceFillOperation(&entry, Data, Objects, EventKind, FALSE);
   498	    entry.MmDoesResult = SAFEUPLOAD_ADMISSION_TRACE_MMDOES_SKIPPED;
   499	    SafeUploadStageAdmissionTraceRecord(&entry);
   500	    SafeUploadStageAdmissionTraceEnd();
   501	}
   502	
   503	// Observe-only. With the file-lifetime option on, remembers the stream of every writable CreateSection and
   504	// records that event even when the (noisy) section option is off, so cleanup/close of the file objects that
   505	// stream's section keeps alive can be attributed to it.
   506	static VOID StageTraceWritableCreateSection(
   507	    _In_ PFLT_CALLBACK_DATA Data,
   508	    _In_ PCFLT_RELATED_OBJECTS Objects)
   509	{
   510	    LONG traceState = SafeUploadAdmissionTraceControlState;
   511	    PFILE_OBJECT fileObject = Data->Iopb->TargetFileObject;
   512	    SAFEUPLOAD_ADMISSION_TRACE_ENTRY entry;
   513	    UINT32 protection;
   514	
   515	    if ((traceState & 1) == 0 || AdmissionTraceLifetimeEvents == 0 || fileObject == NULL) return;
   516	    if (Data->Iopb->Parameters.AcquireForSectionSynchronization.SyncType != SyncTypeCreateSection) return;
   517	    protection = Data->Iopb->Parameters.AcquireForSectionSynchronization.PageProtection;
   518	    if ((protection & (PAGE_READWRITE | PAGE_WRITECOPY | PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE | PAGE_EXECUTE_WRITECOPY)) == 0) return;
   519	    StageTraceWatchAdd(fileObject->SectionObjectPointer);
   520	    if (SafeUploadAdmissionTraceSectionEvents != 0) return;   // the section hook records it
   521	    if (!SafeUploadStageAdmissionTraceBegin(traceState)) return;
   522	    StageAdmissionTraceFillOperation(&entry, Data, Objects, SAFEUPLOAD_ADMISSION_TRACE_EVENT_SECTION_ACQUIRE, FALSE);
   523	    entry.MmDoesResult = SAFEUPLOAD_ADMISSION_TRACE_MMDOES_SKIPPED;
   524	    entry.SyncType = (UINT32)Data->Iopb->Parameters.AcquireForSectionSynchronization.SyncType;
   525	    entry.PageProtection = protection;
   526	    entry.SyncParametersValid = 1;
   527	    SafeUploadStageAdmissionTraceRecord(&entry);
   528	    SafeUploadStageAdmissionTraceEnd();
   529	}
   530	
   531	
   532	static NTSTATUS StageAdmissionProbeWorkerBody(
   533	    _In_ PCUNICODE_STRING VolumeName,
   534	    _In_ PCUNICODE_STRING RelativePath)
   535	{
   536	    SAFEUPLOAD_ADMISSION_TRACE_ENTRY entry;
   537	    OBJECT_ATTRIBUTES objectAttributes;
   538	    IO_STATUS_BLOCK ioStatusBlock;
   539	    UNICODE_STRING fullPath;
   540	    PFLT_VOLUME volume = NULL;
   541	    PFLT_INSTANCE instance = NULL;
   542	    PFLT_CONTEXT instanceContext = NULL;
   543	    HANDLE probeHandle = NULL;
   544	    PFILE_OBJECT probeFileObject = NULL;
   545	    HANDLE identityHandle = NULL;
   546	    PFILE_OBJECT identityObject = NULL;
   547	    PSECTION_OBJECT_POINTERS sectionObjectPointer = NULL;
   548	    PWCHAR fullPathBuffer = NULL;
   549	    FLT_FILESYSTEM_TYPE fileSystemType;
   550	    SAFEUPLOAD_VOLUME_KIND volumeKind = SafeUploadVolumeUnknown;
   551	    ULONG fullPathBytes;
   552	    LONG traceState;
   553	    UINT32 probeStage;
   554	    NTSTATUS status;
   555	
   556	    PAGED_CODE();
   557	    NT_ASSERT(KeGetCurrentIrql() == PASSIVE_LEVEL);
   558	
   559	    traceState = InterlockedCompareExchange(&SafeUploadAdmissionTraceControlState, 0, 0);
   560	    if ((traceState & 1) == 0) return STATUS_DEVICE_NOT_READY;
   561	
   562	    RtlZeroMemory(&entry, sizeof(entry));
   563	    entry.EventKind = SAFEUPLOAD_ADMISSION_TRACE_EVENT_EXPLICIT_PROBE;
   564	    entry.ProcessId = HandleToULong(PsGetCurrentProcessId());
   565	    entry.Irql = KeGetCurrentIrql();
   566	    entry.MmDoesResult = SAFEUPLOAD_ADMISSION_TRACE_MMDOES_SKIPPED;
   567	    entry.StreamContextState = SAFEUPLOAD_ADMISSION_TRACE_CONTEXT_NOT_APPLICABLE;
   568	    entry.VolumeKind = SafeUploadVolumeUnknown;
   569	    entry.ProbeStage = SAFEUPLOAD_ADMISSION_PROBE_STAGE_VOLUME_LOOKUP;
   570	    probeStage = SAFEUPLOAD_ADMISSION_PROBE_STAGE_VOLUME_LOOKUP;
   571	
   572	    status = FltGetVolumeFromName(SafeUploadData.Filter, VolumeName, &volume);
   573	    if (!NT_SUCCESS(status)) goto Record;
   574	
   575	    probeStage = SAFEUPLOAD_ADMISSION_PROBE_STAGE_INSTANCE_LOOKUP;
   576	    status = FltGetVolumeInstanceFromName(SafeUploadData.Filter, volume, NULL, &instance);
   577	    if (!NT_SUCCESS(status)) goto Record;
   578	    entry.Instance = (UINT64)(ULONG_PTR)instance;
   579	
   580	    probeStage = SAFEUPLOAD_ADMISSION_PROBE_STAGE_INSTANCE_CONTEXT;
   581	    status = FltGetInstanceContext(instance, &instanceContext);
   582	    if (!NT_SUCCESS(status)) goto Record;
   583	    volumeKind = ((PSAFEUPLOAD_INSTANCE_CONTEXT)instanceContext)->VolumeKind;
   584	    entry.CanaryState = (UINT32)InterlockedCompareExchange(
   585	        &((PSAFEUPLOAD_INSTANCE_CONTEXT)instanceContext)->CanaryState, 0, 0);
   586	    if (entry.CanaryState >= SAFEUPLOAD_CANARY_PASSED) {
   587	        entry.CanaryStatus = (UINT32)((PSAFEUPLOAD_INSTANCE_CONTEXT)instanceContext)->CanaryStatus;
   588	        entry.CanaryChecks = ((PSAFEUPLOAD_INSTANCE_CONTEXT)instanceContext)->CanaryChecks;
   589	        entry.CanaryCleanupStatus = (UINT32)((PSAFEUPLOAD_INSTANCE_CONTEXT)instanceContext)->CanaryCleanupStatus;
   590	    } else {
   591	        entry.CanaryStatus = entry.CanaryCleanupStatus = (UINT32)STATUS_PENDING;
   592	    }
   593	    entry.VolumeKind = (UINT32)volumeKind;
   594	    FltReleaseContext(instanceContext);
   595	    instanceContext = NULL;
   596	
   597	    if (volumeKind != SafeUploadVolumeFixed) {
   598	        probeStage = SAFEUPLOAD_ADMISSION_PROBE_STAGE_VOLUME_KIND;
   599	        status = STATUS_NOT_SUPPORTED;
   600	        goto Record;
   601	    }
   602	
   603	    probeStage = SAFEUPLOAD_ADMISSION_PROBE_STAGE_FILESYSTEM_TYPE;
   604	    status = FltGetFileSystemType(instance, &fileSystemType);
   605	    if (!NT_SUCCESS(status)) goto Record;
   606	    if (fileSystemType != FLT_FSTYPE_NTFS) {
   607	        status = STATUS_NOT_SUPPORTED;
   608	        goto Record;
   609	    }
   610	
   611	    if (InterlockedCompareExchange(&SafeUploadAdmissionTraceControlState, 0, 0) != traceState) {
   612	        status = STATUS_DEVICE_NOT_READY;
   613	        goto Cleanup;
   614	    }
   615	
   616	    fullPathBytes = (ULONG)VolumeName->Length + (ULONG)RelativePath->Length;
   617	    probeStage = SAFEUPLOAD_ADMISSION_PROBE_STAGE_PATH_ALLOCATION;
   618	    fullPathBuffer = (PWCHAR)ExAllocatePool2(POOL_FLAG_PAGED, fullPathBytes, SAFEUPLOAD_POOL_TAG);
   619	    if (fullPathBuffer == NULL) {
   620	        status = STATUS_INSUFFICIENT_RESOURCES;
   621	        goto Record;
   622	    }
   623	
   624	    RtlCopyMemory(fullPathBuffer, VolumeName->Buffer, VolumeName->Length);
   625	    RtlCopyMemory((PUCHAR)fullPathBuffer + VolumeName->Length,
   626	                  RelativePath->Buffer,
   627	                  RelativePath->Length);
   628	    fullPath.Buffer = fullPathBuffer;
   629	    fullPath.Length = (USHORT)fullPathBytes;
   630	    fullPath.MaximumLength = (USHORT)fullPathBytes;
   631	
   632	    RtlZeroMemory(&ioStatusBlock, sizeof(ioStatusBlock));
   633	    InitializeObjectAttributes(&objectAttributes,
   634	        &fullPath,
   635	        OBJ_KERNEL_HANDLE | OBJ_CASE_INSENSITIVE,
   636	        NULL,
   637	        NULL);
   638	
   639	    probeStage = SAFEUPLOAD_ADMISSION_PROBE_STAGE_LOWER_OPEN;
   640	    status = FltCreateFileEx2(SafeUploadData.Filter,
   641	        instance,
   642	        &probeHandle,
   643	        &probeFileObject,
   644	        FILE_READ_ATTRIBUTES,
   645	        &objectAttributes,
   646	        &ioStatusBlock,
   647	        NULL,
   648	        FILE_ATTRIBUTE_NORMAL,
   649	        FILE_SHARE_READ | FILE_SHARE_WRITE | FILE_SHARE_DELETE,
   650	        FILE_OPEN,
   651	        FILE_NON_DIRECTORY_FILE | FILE_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT | FILE_COMPLETE_IF_OPLOCKED,
   652	        NULL,
   653	        0,
   654	        IO_STOP_ON_SYMLINK,
   655	        NULL);
   656	    if (status == STATUS_STOPPED_ON_SYMLINK && ioStatusBlock.Information != 0)
   657	        ExFreePool((PVOID)ioStatusBlock.Information);
   658	    if (status != STATUS_SUCCESS) goto Record;
   659	    status = SafeUploadStageOpenByIdentity(instance, VolumeName, probeFileObject,
   660	        &identityHandle, &identityObject, &probeStage);
   661	    if (status != STATUS_SUCCESS) goto Record;
   662	    entry.ProbeStatus = (UINT32)status;
   663	    if (identityObject != NULL) {
   664	        sectionObjectPointer = identityObject->SectionObjectPointer;
   665	        entry.TargetFileObject = (UINT64)(ULONG_PTR)identityObject;
   666	        entry.SectionObjectPointer = (UINT64)(ULONG_PTR)sectionObjectPointer;
   667	    }
   668	    if (sectionObjectPointer != NULL && KeGetCurrentIrql() == PASSIVE_LEVEL) {
   669	        entry.MmDoesResult = MmDoesFileHaveUserWritableReferences(sectionObjectPointer) ?
   670	            SAFEUPLOAD_ADMISSION_TRACE_MMDOES_YES :
   671	            SAFEUPLOAD_ADMISSION_TRACE_MMDOES_NO;
   672	    }
   673	    if (identityObject != NULL) {
   674	        /* Reuses AdmissionRecordState (a constant until now): H(F) of the probed stream, bit 31 = untracked. */
   675	        entry.AdmissionRecordState = SafeUploadStageWritersSnapshot(instance, identityObject);
   676	        /* SetupFlags is unused by probe entries; it carries C(F) of the probed stream. */
   677	        entry.SetupFlags = SafeUploadStageSectionsInFlight(sectionObjectPointer);
   678	    }
   679	    if (sectionObjectPointer == NULL) status = STATUS_INVALID_FILE_FOR_SECTION;
   680	    if (status == STATUS_SUCCESS) probeStage = SAFEUPLOAD_ADMISSION_PROBE_STAGE_COMPLETE;
   681	
   682	Record:
   683	    entry.ProbeStage = probeStage;
   684	    entry.ProbeStatus = (UINT32)status;
   685	    if (!SafeUploadStageAdmissionTraceBegin(traceState)) {
   686	        status = STATUS_DEVICE_NOT_READY;
   687	        goto Cleanup;
   688	    }
   689	    (VOID)StageAdmissionTraceRecordInternal(&entry, TRUE);
   690	    SafeUploadStageAdmissionTraceEnd();
   691	    status = STATUS_SUCCESS;
   692	
   693	Cleanup:
   694	    if (identityHandle != NULL) FltClose(identityHandle);
   695	    if (identityObject != NULL) ObDereferenceObject(identityObject);
   696	    if (instanceContext != NULL) FltReleaseContext(instanceContext);
   697	    if (fullPathBuffer != NULL) ExFreePoolWithTag(fullPathBuffer, SAFEUPLOAD_POOL_TAG);
   698	    if (probeHandle != NULL) FltClose(probeHandle);
   699	    if (probeFileObject != NULL) ObDereferenceObject(probeFileObject);
   700	    if (instance != NULL) FltObjectDereference(instance);
   701	    if (volume != NULL) FltObjectDereference(volume);
   702	    return status;
   703	}
   704	
   705	NTSTATUS SafeUploadStageAdmissionDeleteStreamContext(_In_ PCUNICODE_STRING VolumeName,
   706	    _In_ PCUNICODE_STRING RelativePath)
   707	{
   708	    PFLT_VOLUME volume = NULL;
   709	    PFLT_VOLUME cVolume = NULL;
   710	    PFLT_INSTANCE instance = NULL;
   711	    PSAFEUPLOAD_INSTANCE_CONTEXT instanceContext = NULL;
   712	    PSAFEUPLOAD_STREAM_CONTEXT streamContext = NULL;
   713	    HANDLE handle = NULL;
   714	    PFILE_OBJECT fileObject = NULL;
   715	    PWCHAR fullPathBuffer = NULL;
   716	    UNICODE_STRING fullPath;
   717	    OBJECT_ATTRIBUTES attributes;
   718	    IO_STATUS_BLOCK ioStatus;
   719	    FLT_FILESYSTEM_TYPE fileSystemType;
   720	    ULONG fullPathBytes;
   721	    NTSTATUS status;
   722	    UNICODE_STRING cDrive = RTL_CONSTANT_STRING(L"\\??\\C:");
   723	
   724	    NT_ASSERT(KeGetCurrentIrql() == PASSIVE_LEVEL);
   725	
   726	    if (VolumeName == NULL || RelativePath == NULL || VolumeName->Length == 0 ||
   727	        RelativePath->Length < sizeof(WCHAR) * 2 ||
   728	        VolumeName->Length > MAXUSHORT - RelativePath->Length ||
   729	        (VolumeName->Length % sizeof(WCHAR)) != 0 || (RelativePath->Length % sizeof(WCHAR)) != 0) {
   730	        return STATUS_INVALID_PARAMETER;
   731	    }
   732	
   733	    status = FltGetVolumeFromName(SafeUploadData.Filter, VolumeName, &volume);
   734	    if (!NT_SUCCESS(status)) goto Exit;
   735	    status = FltGetVolumeFromName(SafeUploadData.Filter, &cDrive, &cVolume);
   736	    if (!NT_SUCCESS(status)) goto Exit;
   737	    if (cVolume != volume) {
   738	        status = STATUS_INVALID_PARAMETER;
   739	        goto Exit;
   740	    }
   741	    status = FltGetVolumeInstanceFromName(SafeUploadData.Filter, volume, NULL, &instance);
   742	    if (!NT_SUCCESS(status)) goto Exit;
   743	
   744	    status = FltGetInstanceContext(instance, (PFLT_CONTEXT *)&instanceContext);
   745	    if (!NT_SUCCESS(status)) goto Exit;
   746	    if (instanceContext->VolumeKind != SafeUploadVolumeFixed) {
   747	        status = STATUS_NOT_SUPPORTED;
   748	        goto Exit;
   749	    }
   750	    status = FltGetFileSystemType(instance, &fileSystemType);
   751	    if (!NT_SUCCESS(status)) goto Exit;
   752	    if (fileSystemType != FLT_FSTYPE_NTFS) {
   753	        status = STATUS_NOT_SUPPORTED;
   754	        goto Exit;
   755	    }
   756	    FltReleaseContext(instanceContext);
   757	    instanceContext = NULL;
   758	
   759	    fullPathBytes = (ULONG)VolumeName->Length + (ULONG)RelativePath->Length;
   760	    fullPathBuffer = (PWCHAR)ExAllocatePool2(POOL_FLAG_PAGED, fullPathBytes, SAFEUPLOAD_POOL_TAG);
   761	    if (fullPathBuffer == NULL) {
   762	        status = STATUS_INSUFFICIENT_RESOURCES;
   763	        goto Exit;
   764	    }
   765	    /* Bounded copies: MaximumLength is exactly the validated sum, so both appends must fit. */
   766	    fullPath.Buffer = fullPathBuffer;
   767	    fullPath.Length = 0;
   768	    fullPath.MaximumLength = (USHORT)fullPathBytes;
   769	    RtlCopyUnicodeString(&fullPath, VolumeName);
   770	    status = RtlAppendUnicodeStringToString(&fullPath, RelativePath);
   771	    if (!NT_SUCCESS(status) || fullPath.Length != fullPathBytes) {
   772	        status = STATUS_INVALID_PARAMETER;
   773	        goto Exit;
   774	    }
   775	
   776	    InitializeObjectAttributes(&attributes, &fullPath,
   777	        OBJ_KERNEL_HANDLE | OBJ_CASE_INSENSITIVE, NULL, NULL);
   778	    RtlZeroMemory(&ioStatus, sizeof(ioStatus));
   779	    status = FltCreateFileEx2(SafeUploadData.Filter, instance, &handle, &fileObject,
   780	        FILE_READ_ATTRIBUTES, &attributes, &ioStatus, NULL, FILE_ATTRIBUTE_NORMAL,
   781	        FILE_SHARE_READ | FILE_SHARE_WRITE | FILE_SHARE_DELETE, FILE_OPEN,
   782	        FILE_NON_DIRECTORY_FILE | FILE_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT | FILE_COMPLETE_IF_OPLOCKED,
   783	        NULL, 0, IO_STOP_ON_SYMLINK, NULL);
   784	    if (status == STATUS_STOPPED_ON_SYMLINK && ioStatus.Information != 0)
   785	        ExFreePool((PVOID)ioStatus.Information);
   786	    if (!NT_SUCCESS(status)) goto Exit;
   787	
   788	    status = FltGetStreamContext(instance, fileObject, (PFLT_CONTEXT *)&streamContext);
   789	    if (!NT_SUCCESS(status)) goto Exit;
   790	    if ((SafeUploadStageWritersSnapshot(instance, fileObject) &
   791	         ~SAFEUPLOAD_WRITERS_UNTRACKED_BIT) == 0) {
   792	        status = STATUS_INVALID_DEVICE_STATE;
   793	        goto Exit;
   794	    }
   795	
   796	    FltDeleteContext((PFLT_CONTEXT)streamContext);
   797	    status = STATUS_SUCCESS;
   798	
   799	Exit:
   800	    if (streamContext != NULL) FltReleaseContext(streamContext);

 static FLT_PREOP_CALLBACK_STATUS StagePreOperation(PFLT_CALLBACK_DATA Data,
     PCFLT_RELATED_OBJECTS Objects, PVOID *CompletionContext, PVOID AdmissionToken);
 
 static PSTAGE_STREAM StageStreamForObject(PFILE_OBJECT FileObject)
 {
     PLIST_ENTRY link;
     KIRQL irql;
     PSTAGE_STREAM found = NULL;
     if (FileObject == NULL || FileObject->FsContext == NULL) return NULL;
     StageStreamAcquireSpinLock(&StageListLock, &irql);
     for (link = StageStreams.Flink; link != &StageStreams; link = link->Flink) {
         PSTAGE_STREAM stream = CONTAINING_RECORD(link, STAGE_STREAM, Link);
         if (FileObject->FsContext == &stream->Header) { found = stream; break; }
     }
     StageStreamReleaseSpinLock(&StageListLock, irql);
     /* Streams remain allocated until unregister has drained all callbacks. */
     return found;
 }
 
 static VOID StageAcquire(PERESOURCE Resource)
 {
     KeEnterCriticalRegion();
     ExAcquireResourceExclusiveLite(Resource, TRUE);
 }
 
 static VOID StageRelease(PERESOURCE Resource)
 {
     ExReleaseResourceLite(Resource);
     KeLeaveCriticalRegion();
 }
 
 static BOOLEAN StageCacheAcquire(PVOID Context, BOOLEAN Wait)
 {
     PSTAGE_STREAM stream = Context;
     KeEnterCriticalRegion();
     if (!ExAcquireResourceSharedLite(&stream->PagingResource, Wait)) {
         KeLeaveCriticalRegion();
         return FALSE;
     }
     return TRUE;
 }
 
 static VOID StageCacheRelease(PVOID Context)
 {
     PSTAGE_STREAM stream = Context;
     StageRelease(&stream->PagingResource);
 }
 
 static CACHE_MANAGER_CALLBACKS StageCacheCallbacks = {
     StageCacheAcquire, StageCacheRelease, StageCacheAcquire, StageCacheRelease
 };
 
 static VOID StageInitializeCache(PSTAGE_STREAM Stream, PFILE_OBJECT FileObject)
 {
     CC_FILE_SIZES sizes;
     if (FileObject->PrivateCacheMap != NULL) return;
     sizes.AllocationSize = Stream->Header.AllocationSize;
     sizes.FileSize = Stream->Header.FileSize;
     sizes.ValidDataLength = Stream->Header.ValidDataLength;
     CcInitializeCacheMap(FileObject, &sizes, FALSE, &StageCacheCallbacks, Stream);
 }

+static NTSTATUS StageFlushCache(PSTAGE_STREAM Stream)
 {
     IO_STATUS_BLOCK io = {0};
     if (Stream->Sections.DataSectionObject != NULL) {
         CcFlushCache(&Stream->Sections, NULL, 0, &io);
         if (!NT_SUCCESS(io.Status)) return io.Status;
     }
+    return STATUS_SUCCESS;
+}

+{

 }
 
 static VOID StageFreeStream(PSTAGE_STREAM Stream)
 {
     if (Stream->BackingObject != NULL) ObDereferenceObject(Stream->BackingObject);
     if (Stream->BackingHandle != NULL) FltClose(Stream->BackingHandle);
     if (Stream->BackingInstance != NULL) FltObjectDereference(Stream->BackingInstance);
     if (Stream->OriginalInstance != NULL) FltObjectDereference(Stream->OriginalInstance);
     if (Stream->RenameExchange != NULL) ExFreePoolWithTag(Stream->RenameExchange, STAGE_TAG);
     if (Stream->ByteLocks != NULL) FltFreeFileLock(Stream->ByteLocks);
     if (Stream->PagingInitialized) ExDeleteResourceLite(&Stream->PagingResource);
     if (Stream->ResourceInitialized) ExDeleteResourceLite(&Stream->Resource);
     ExFreePoolWithTag(Stream, STAGE_TAG);
 }

 static NTSTATUS StageOpenBacking(PSTAGE_STREAM Stream, BOOLEAN ReadOnly)
 {
     UNICODE_STRING drive = RTL_CONSTANT_STRING(L"\\??\\C:");
     PFLT_VOLUME volume = NULL, sourceVolume = NULL;
     PDEVICE_OBJECT sourceDevice = NULL, backingDevice = NULL;
     FLT_VOLUME_PROPERTIES properties;
     ULONG needed;
     OBJECT_ATTRIBUTES attributes;
     IO_STATUS_BLOCK io;
     FILE_STANDARD_INFORMATION standard;
     FLT_FILESYSTEM_TYPE fs;
     NTSTATUS status;
     if (Stream->BackingInstance == NULL) {
         status = FltGetVolumeFromName(SafeUploadData.Filter, &drive, &volume);
         if (!NT_SUCCESS(status)) goto Exit;
         status = FltGetVolumeProperties(volume, &properties, sizeof(properties), &needed);
         if (status != STATUS_BUFFER_OVERFLOW && !NT_SUCCESS(status)) goto Exit;
         Stream->SectorSize = properties.SectorSize;
         if (Stream->SectorSize < 512 || Stream->SectorSize > 65536 ||
             (Stream->SectorSize & (Stream->SectorSize - 1)) != 0) {
             status = STATUS_NOT_SUPPORTED; goto Exit;
         }
         status = FltGetVolumeInstanceFromName(SafeUploadData.Filter, volume, NULL, &Stream->BackingInstance);
         if (!NT_SUCCESS(status)) goto Exit;
         status = FltGetFileSystemType(Stream->BackingInstance, &fs);
         if (!NT_SUCCESS(status)) goto Exit;
         if (fs != FLT_FSTYPE_NTFS) { status = STATUS_NOT_SUPPORTED; goto Exit; }
         status = FltGetVolumeFromInstance(Stream->OriginalInstance, &sourceVolume);
         if (!NT_SUCCESS(status)) goto Exit;
         status = FltGetDeviceObject(sourceVolume, &sourceDevice);
         if (!NT_SUCCESS(status)) goto Exit;
         status = FltGetDeviceObject(volume, &backingDevice);
         if (!NT_SUCCESS(status)) goto Exit;
         if (backingDevice->StackSize < sourceDevice->StackSize) {
             status = STATUS_NOT_SUPPORTED; goto Exit;
         }
     }
     InitializeObjectAttributes(&attributes, &Stream->StageName,
         OBJ_KERNEL_HANDLE | OBJ_CASE_INSENSITIVE, NULL, NULL);
     status = FltCreateFileEx2(SafeUploadData.Filter, Stream->BackingInstance,
         &Stream->BackingHandle, &Stream->BackingObject,
         FILE_READ_DATA | FILE_READ_ATTRIBUTES | SYNCHRONIZE |
             (ReadOnly ? 0 : FILE_WRITE_DATA | FILE_WRITE_ATTRIBUTES),
         &attributes, &io, NULL, FILE_ATTRIBUTE_NORMAL, ReadOnly ? FILE_SHARE_READ : 0, FILE_OPEN,
         FILE_NON_DIRECTORY_FILE | FILE_SYNCHRONOUS_IO_NONALERT | FILE_NO_INTERMEDIATE_BUFFERING,
         NULL, 0, IO_STOP_ON_SYMLINK, NULL);
     if (status == STATUS_STOPPED_ON_SYMLINK && io.Information != 0) ExFreePool((PVOID)io.Information);
     if (!NT_SUCCESS(status)) goto Exit;
     if (!ReadOnly && Stream->BackingObject->SectionObjectPointer != NULL &&
         (Stream->BackingObject->SectionObjectPointer->SharedCacheMap != NULL ||
          Stream->BackingObject->SectionObjectPointer->DataSectionObject != NULL ||
          Stream->BackingObject->SectionObjectPointer->ImageSectionObject != NULL)) {
         /* Do not purge or alter NTFS's cache. The allocator must hand over an
          * uncached backing, and the exclusive writer prevents new readers. */
         status = STATUS_USER_MAPPED_FILE;
         goto Exit;
     }
     if (!ReadOnly) {
         PFLT_FILE_NAME_INFORMATION name = NULL;
         status = FltGetFileNameInformationUnsafe(Stream->BackingObject, Stream->BackingInstance,
             FLT_FILE_NAME_NORMALIZED | FLT_FILE_NAME_QUERY_FILESYSTEM_ONLY, &name);
@@ -1413,161 +1420,165 @@ static NTSTATUS StageCreate(PFLT_CALLBACK_DATA Data, PCFLT_RELATED_OBJECTS Objec
             status = StageSetIdentity(view, basename);
             if (!NT_SUCCESS(status)) goto Exit;
         }
         status = RtlStringCchPrintfW(stream->StageBuffer, SAFEUPLOAD_MAX_PATH_CHARS,
             L"\\??\\C:\\ProgramData\\SafeUpload\\staging\\%ws", basename);
         if (!NT_SUCCESS(status)) goto Exit;
         RtlInitUnicodeString(&stream->StageName, stream->StageBuffer);

     }
     if (FLT_IS_IRP_OPERATION(Data) && FltIsIoCanceled(Data)) {
         status = STATUS_CANCELLED; goto Exit;
     }
     handle = ExAllocatePool2(POOL_FLAG_NON_PAGED, sizeof(*handle), STAGE_TAG);
     if (handle == NULL) { status = STATUS_INSUFFICIENT_RESOURCES; goto Exit; }
     StageAcquire(&stream->Resource);
     status = IoCheckShareAccess(granted, Data->Iopb->Parameters.Create.ShareAccess,
         file, &stream->ShareAccess, FALSE);
     if (NT_SUCCESS(status) && !newStream && Writer &&
         (disposition == FILE_OVERWRITE || disposition == FILE_OVERWRITE_IF || disposition == FILE_SUPERSEDE)) {
         LARGE_INTEGER zero = {0};
         /* CcSetFileSizes needs a file object bound to this stream. */
         file->FsContext = &stream->Header;
         file->SectionObjectPointer = &stream->Sections;
         __try { status = StageResize(stream, file, zero); }
         __except(EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER) { status = GetExceptionCode(); }
         if (!NT_SUCCESS(status)) {
             (VOID)CcUninitializeCacheMap(file, NULL, NULL);
             file->FsContext = NULL; file->SectionObjectPointer = NULL;
         }
     }
     if (NT_SUCCESS(status)) {
         IoUpdateShareAccess(file, &stream->ShareAccess);
         handle->Stream = stream; handle->GrantedAccess = granted;
         file->FsContext = &stream->Header;
         file->FsContext2 = handle;
         file->SectionObjectPointer = &stream->Sections;
         SetFlag(file->Flags, FO_CACHE_SUPPORTED);
         InterlockedIncrement(&stream->FileObjects);
         InterlockedIncrement(&StageFileObjects);
         Data->Iopb->Parameters.Create.SecurityContext->AccessState->PreviouslyGrantedAccess |= granted;
         Data->Iopb->Parameters.Create.SecurityContext->AccessState->RemainingDesiredAccess &= ~(granted | MAXIMUM_ALLOWED);
         information = !exists ? FILE_CREATED : disposition == FILE_SUPERSEDE ? FILE_SUPERSEDED :
             (disposition == FILE_OVERWRITE || disposition == FILE_OVERWRITE_IF) ? FILE_OVERWRITTEN : FILE_OPENED;
         Data->IoStatus.Information = information;
     }
     StageRelease(&stream->Resource);
     if (!NT_SUCCESS(status)) goto Exit;
     if (newStream) {
         StageStreamAcquireSpinLock(&StageListLock, &irql);
         InsertTailList(&StageStreams, &stream->Link);
         StageStreamReleaseSpinLock(&StageListLock, irql);
         StageStreamCount++;
         view->Current = stream;
     }
     if (newView) InsertTailList(&StageViews, &view->Link);
 Exit:
     if (!NT_SUCCESS(status)) {
         if (handle != NULL) ExFreePoolWithTag(handle, STAGE_TAG);
         if (newStream) StageFreeStream(stream);
         if (newView) StageFreeView(view);
         /* A durable allocation whose CREATE failed remains unsealed for recovery. */
     }
     StageRelease(&StageNamespaceResource);
     return status;
 }
 
 
 /* Materialize the extended range with ordinary noncached backing writes.
  * Paging writes do not advance NTFS's valid data length. Claiming a larger
  * upper VDL without zeroing could expose old disk contents when Cc advances it.
  * Preserve an existing partial sector; the backing has no second data cache. */
 static NTSTATUS StageZeroGrowth(PSTAGE_STREAM Stream, LARGE_INTEGER Size)
 {
     PVOID buffer;
     LARGE_INTEGER offset;
     LONGLONG rounded = (Size.QuadPart + Stream->SectorSize - 1) & ~((LONGLONG)Stream->SectorSize - 1);
     ULONG length, transferred, tail;
     NTSTATUS status;
     if (Size.QuadPart <= Stream->Header.FileSize.QuadPart) return STATUS_SUCCESS;
-    status = StageFlush(Stream);
+    /* Preserve existing upper dirty bytes before reading the partial tail.
+     * The noncached lower object has no data cache to drain. Device durability
+     * belongs to explicit flush/cleanup/seal, not a resize performed by Mm
+     * while creating an extending section. */
+    status = StageFlushCache(Stream);
     if (!NT_SUCCESS(status)) return status;
     buffer = FltAllocatePoolAlignedWithTag(Stream->BackingInstance, NonPagedPoolNx, 65536, STAGE_TAG);
     if (buffer == NULL) return STATUS_INSUFFICIENT_RESOURCES;
     RtlZeroMemory(buffer, 65536);
     offset.QuadPart = Stream->Header.FileSize.QuadPart & ~((LONGLONG)Stream->SectorSize - 1);
     tail = (ULONG)(Stream->Header.FileSize.QuadPart - offset.QuadPart);
     if (tail != 0) {
         status = FltReadFile(Stream->BackingInstance, Stream->BackingObject, &offset,
             Stream->SectorSize, buffer, FLTFL_IO_OPERATION_NON_CACHED |
             FLTFL_IO_OPERATION_DO_NOT_UPDATE_BYTE_OFFSET, &transferred, NULL, NULL);
         if (!NT_SUCCESS(status) || transferred < tail) {
             if (NT_SUCCESS(status)) status = STATUS_UNEXPECTED_IO_ERROR;
             goto Exit;
         }
         RtlZeroMemory((PUCHAR)buffer + tail, 65536 - tail);
     }
     while (offset.QuadPart < rounded) {
         length = (ULONG)min(65536LL, rounded - offset.QuadPart);
         status = FltWriteFile(Stream->BackingInstance, Stream->BackingObject, &offset,
             length, buffer, FLTFL_IO_OPERATION_NON_CACHED |
             FLTFL_IO_OPERATION_DO_NOT_UPDATE_BYTE_OFFSET, &transferred, NULL, NULL);
         if (!NT_SUCCESS(status) || transferred != length) {
             if (NT_SUCCESS(status)) status = STATUS_UNEXPECTED_IO_ERROR;
             goto Exit;
         }
         offset.QuadPart += length;
         if (tail != 0) { RtlZeroMemory(buffer, 65536); tail = 0; }
     }
 Exit:
     FltFreePoolAlignedWithTag(Stream->BackingInstance, buffer, STAGE_TAG);
     return status;
 }
 
 static NTSTATUS StageResize(PSTAGE_STREAM Stream, PFILE_OBJECT FileObject, LARGE_INTEGER Size)
 {
     FILE_END_OF_FILE_INFORMATION end;
     CC_FILE_SIZES sizes;
     NTSTATUS status;
     NT_ASSERT(ExIsResourceAcquiredExclusiveLite(&Stream->Resource));
     if (Stream->ReadOnly || Stream->RenameExchange != NULL) return STATUS_ACCESS_DENIED;
     if (Size.QuadPart < 0 || Size.QuadPart > STAGE_MAX_BYTES) return STATUS_FILE_TOO_LARGE;
     if (Size.QuadPart < Stream->Header.FileSize.QuadPart &&
         !MmCanFileBeTruncated(&Stream->Sections, &Size)) return STATUS_USER_MAPPED_FILE;
     status = StageZeroGrowth(Stream, Size);
     if (!NT_SUCCESS(status)) return status;
     end.EndOfFile = Size;
     status = FltSetInformationFile(Stream->BackingInstance, Stream->BackingObject,
         &end, sizeof(end), FileEndOfFileInformation);
     if (!NT_SUCCESS(status)) return status;
     Stream->Header.FileSize = Size;
     Stream->Header.AllocationSize.QuadPart = (Size.QuadPart + PAGE_SIZE - 1) & ~((LONGLONG)PAGE_SIZE - 1);
     Stream->Header.ValidDataLength = Size; /* Every extended byte was materialized. */
     if (Stream->Sections.SharedCacheMap != NULL) {
         StageInitializeCache(Stream, FileObject);
         sizes.AllocationSize = Stream->Header.AllocationSize;
         sizes.FileSize = Size;
         sizes.ValidDataLength = Size;
         CcSetFileSizes(FileObject, &sizes);
     }
     return STATUS_SUCCESS;
 }
 
 static NTSTATUS StageReadWrite(PFLT_CALLBACK_DATA Data, PSTAGE_STREAM Stream)

     LARGE_INTEGER offset = write ? Data->Iopb->Parameters.Write.ByteOffset : Data->Iopb->Parameters.Read.ByteOffset;
     PFILE_OBJECT file = Data->Iopb->TargetFileObject;
     PMDL mdl = write ? Data->Iopb->Parameters.Write.MdlAddress : Data->Iopb->Parameters.Read.MdlAddress;
     PVOID buffer;
     NTSTATUS status;
     if (write && (Stream->ReadOnly || Stream->RenameExchange != NULL)) return STATUS_ACCESS_DENIED;

     if (mdl == NULL && !FLT_IS_SYSTEM_BUFFER(Data)) {
         status = FltLockUserBuffer(Data);
         if (!NT_SUCCESS(status)) return status;
         mdl = write ? Data->Iopb->Parameters.Write.MdlAddress : Data->Iopb->Parameters.Read.MdlAddress;
     }
     buffer = mdl != NULL ? MmGetSystemAddressForMdlSafe(mdl, NormalPagePriority | MdlMappingNoExecute) :
         write ? Data->Iopb->Parameters.Write.WriteBuffer : Data->Iopb->Parameters.Read.ReadBuffer;

  1712	static BOOLEAN StageExchange(PSAFEUPLOAD_EXCHANGE Exchange)
  1713	{
  1714	    BOOLEAN answered = FALSE;
  1715	    UINT32 verdict;
  1716	    NTSTATUS status = SafeUploadRequestVerdict(Exchange, &verdict, &answered);

  1720	/* Retrying uses the SAME request ID. Until acknowledgement, the namespace is
  1721	 * frozen and retirement is impossible. A lost prepare becomes an abort. */
  1722	static VOID StageFinishRename(PSTAGE_STREAM Stream)
  1723	{
  1724	    if (Stream->RenameExchange != NULL && StageExchange(Stream->RenameExchange)) {
  1725	        PSAFEUPLOAD_EXCHANGE exchange;
  1726	        StageAcquire(&Stream->Resource);
  1727	        exchange = Stream->RenameExchange;
  1728	        Stream->RenameExchange = NULL;
  1729	        StageRelease(&Stream->Resource);
  1730	        ExFreePoolWithTag(exchange, STAGE_TAG);
  1731	    }
  1732	}

  1734	static NTSTATUS StageRename(PFLT_CALLBACK_DATA Data, PSTAGE_STREAM Stream)
  1735	{
  1736	    PFILE_RENAME_INFORMATION rename = Data->Iopb->Parameters.SetFileInformation.InfoBuffer;
  1737	    PFLT_FILE_NAME_INFORMATION destination = NULL;
  1738	    PSTAGE_VIEW view = Stream->View, target = NULL;
  1739	    PSTAGE_OLD_NAME old = NULL;
  1740	    PSAFEUPLOAD_EXCHANGE exchange = NULL;
  1741	    ULONG length = Data->Iopb->Parameters.SetFileInformation.Length;
  1742	    ULONG basename;
  1743	    BOOLEAN extended = Data->Iopb->Parameters.SetFileInformation.FileInformationClass == FileRenameInformationEx;
  1744	    BOOLEAN replace, posix;
  1745	    NTSTATUS status;
  1746	    if (!Data->Iopb->TargetFileObject->DeleteAccess) return STATUS_ACCESS_DENIED;
  1747	    if (rename == NULL || length < (ULONG)FIELD_OFFSET(FILE_RENAME_INFORMATION, FileName) ||
  1748	        rename->FileNameLength == 0 || (rename->FileNameLength & 1) ||
  1749	        rename->FileNameLength > length - (ULONG)FIELD_OFFSET(FILE_RENAME_INFORMATION, FileName))
  1750	        return STATUS_INVALID_PARAMETER;
  1751	    replace = extended ? BooleanFlagOn(*(PULONG)rename, FILE_RENAME_REPLACE_IF_EXISTS) : rename->ReplaceIfExists;
  1752	    posix = extended && BooleanFlagOn(*(PULONG)rename, FILE_RENAME_POSIX_SEMANTICS);
  1753	    if (extended && FlagOn(*(PULONG)rename, ~(FILE_RENAME_REPLACE_IF_EXISTS | FILE_RENAME_POSIX_SEMANTICS)))
  1754	        return STATUS_NOT_SUPPORTED;
  1755	    StageAcquire(&StageNamespaceResource);
  1756	    if (StageStopping || view->Detached || view->Current != Stream || Stream->RenameExchange != NULL ||
  1757	        (Stream->ReadOnly && !Stream->Sealed)) {
  1758	        status = STATUS_SHARING_VIOLATION; goto Exit;
  1759	    }
  1760	    status = FltGetDestinationFileNameInformation(Stream->OriginalInstance,
  1761	        Data->Iopb->TargetFileObject, rename->RootDirectory, rename->FileName,
  1762	        rename->FileNameLength, FLT_FILE_NAME_NORMALIZED | FLT_FILE_NAME_QUERY_FILESYSTEM_ONLY |
  1763	            FLT_FILE_NAME_REQUEST_FROM_CURRENT_PROVIDER, &destination);
  1764	    if (!NT_SUCCESS(status)) goto Exit;
  1765	    status = FltParseFileNameInformation(destination);
  1766	    if (!NT_SUCCESS(status)) goto Exit;
  1767	    if (destination->Name.Length > sizeof(Stream->NameBuffer) || destination->Stream.Length != 0) {
  1768	        status = STATUS_NOT_SUPPORTED; goto Exit;
  1769	    }
  1770	    if (destination->Volume.Length != Stream->VolumeLength ||
  1771	        RtlCompareMemory(Stream->Name.Buffer, destination->Volume.Buffer, Stream->VolumeLength) != Stream->VolumeLength) {
  1772	        status = STATUS_NOT_SAME_DEVICE; goto Exit;
  1773	    }
  1774	    if (RtlEqualUnicodeString(&view->Name, &destination->Name, TRUE)) { status = STATUS_SUCCESS; goto Exit; }
  1775	    target = StageFindView(view->Owner, &destination->Name);
  1776	    if (target != NULL && !replace) { status = STATUS_OBJECT_NAME_COLLISION; goto Exit; }
  1777	    StageAcquire(&Stream->Resource);
  1778	    status = Stream->ShareAccess.OpenCount == Stream->ShareAccess.SharedDelete ?
  1779	        STATUS_SUCCESS : STATUS_SHARING_VIOLATION;
  1780	    StageRelease(&Stream->Resource);
  1781	    if (!NT_SUCCESS(status)) goto Exit;
  1782	    if (target != NULL) {
  1783	        PSTAGE_STREAM replaced = target->Current;
  1784	        status = SafeUploadStageCheckSubjectAccess(Data, target->Security, DELETE);
  1785	        if (!NT_SUCCESS(status)) goto Exit;
  1786	        StageAcquire(&replaced->Resource);
  1787	        if (replaced->RenameExchange != NULL || (replaced->ReadOnly && !replaced->Sealed) ||
  1788	            (!posix && replaced->ShareAccess.OpenCount != 0) ||
  1789	            replaced->ShareAccess.OpenCount != replaced->ShareAccess.SharedDelete ||
  1790	            replaced->Sections.ImageSectionObject != NULL)
  1791	            status = STATUS_SHARING_VIOLATION;
  1792	        StageRelease(&replaced->Resource);
  1793	        if (!NT_SUCCESS(status)) goto Exit;
  1794	    }
  1795	    status = SafeUploadStageCheckRenameAccess(Data, Stream->OriginalInstance, &destination->Name, replace);
  1796	    if (!NT_SUCCESS(status)) goto Exit;
  1797	    old = ExAllocatePool2(POOL_FLAG_NON_PAGED, sizeof(*old) + view->Name.Length, STAGE_TAG);
  1798	    exchange = ExAllocatePool2(POOL_FLAG_NON_PAGED, sizeof(*exchange), STAGE_TAG);
  1799	    if (old == NULL || exchange == NULL) { status = STATUS_INSUFFICIENT_RESOURCES; goto Exit; }
  1800	    old->Name.Buffer = (PWCH)(old + 1);
  1801	    old->Name.Length = old->Name.MaximumLength = view->Name.Length;
  1802	    RtlCopyMemory(old->Name.Buffer, view->Name.Buffer, view->Name.Length);
  1803	    exchange->Request.Version = SAFEUPLOAD_PROTOCOL_VERSION;
  1804	    exchange->Request.StructSize = sizeof(SAFEUPLOAD_REQUEST);
  1805	    exchange->Request.RequestId = (UINT64)InterlockedIncrement64(&SafeUploadData.NextRequestId);
  1806	    exchange->Request.Operation = SAFEUPLOAD_OPERATION_STAGE_RENAME;
  1807	    exchange->Request.RequestorProcessId = view->OwnerProcessId;
  1808	    exchange->Request.Reserved = Stream->Sealed ? 1 : 0;
  1809	    exchange->Request.PathLength = destination->Name.Length;
  1810	    RtlCopyMemory(exchange->Request.Path, destination->Name.Buffer, destination->Name.Length);
  1811	    basename = Stream->StageName.Length / sizeof(WCHAR);
  1812	    while (basename > 0 && Stream->StageName.Buffer[basename - 1] != L'\\') --basename;
  1813	    exchange->Request.ImageNameLength = 32 * sizeof(WCHAR);
  1814	    RtlCopyMemory(exchange->Request.ImageName, Stream->StageName.Buffer + basename, 32 * sizeof(WCHAR));
  1815	    StageAcquire(&Stream->Resource);
  1816	    Stream->RenameExchange = exchange;
  1817	    StageRelease(&Stream->Resource);
  1818	    exchange = NULL; /* Stream owns uncertain transactions through disconnect. */
  1819	    if (!StageExchange(Stream->RenameExchange)) {
  1820	        Stream->RenameExchange->Request.Operation = SAFEUPLOAD_OPERATION_STAGE_RENAME_ABORT;
  1821	        status = STATUS_SHARING_VIOLATION; goto Exit;
  1822	    }
  1823	    StageAcquire(&Stream->Resource);
  1824	    if (target != NULL) target->Detached = TRUE;
  1825	    RtlCopyUnicodeString(&Stream->Name, &destination->Name);
  1826	    RtlCopyUnicodeString(&view->Name, &destination->Name);
  1827	    old->Sequence = ++StageNamespaceSequence; /* Caller owns namespace resource. */
  1828	    old->Version = Stream;
  1829	    InsertTailList(&view->OldNames, &old->Link); old = NULL;
  1830	    StageRelease(&Stream->Resource);
  1831	    Stream->RenameExchange->Request.Operation = SAFEUPLOAD_OPERATION_STAGE_RENAME_COMMIT;
  1832	    StageFinishRename(Stream);
  1833	    (VOID)FltPurgeFileNameInformationCache(Stream->OriginalInstance, NULL);
  1834	    status = STATUS_SUCCESS;
  1835	Exit:
  1836	    if (exchange != NULL) ExFreePoolWithTag(exchange, STAGE_TAG);
  1837	    if (old != NULL) ExFreePoolWithTag(old, STAGE_TAG);
  1838	    if (destination != NULL) FltReleaseFileNameInformation(destination);
  1839	    StageRelease(&StageNamespaceResource);
  1840	    return status;
  1841	}
  1842	
  1843	typedef struct _STAGE_PAGING_IO {
  1844	    PSTAGE_STREAM Stream;
  1845	    PFLT_CALLBACK_DATA Original;
  1846	    PVOID AdmissionToken;
  1847	    volatile LONG References; /* Submission and completion own one each. */
  1848	    volatile LONG State;      /* 0 submitting, 1 pended, 2 completed inline. */
  1849	} STAGE_PAGING_IO, *PSTAGE_PAGING_IO;
  1850	
  1851	static NTSTATUS StageClonePagingMdls(PMDL Source, PMDL *Target)
  1852	{
  1853	    *Target = NULL;
  1854	    while (Source != NULL) {
  1855	        PMDL mdl = IoAllocateMdl(MmGetMdlVirtualAddress(Source),
  1856	            MmGetMdlByteCount(Source), FALSE, FALSE, NULL);
  1857	        if (mdl == NULL) return STATUS_INSUFFICIENT_RESOURCES;
  1858	        IoBuildPartialMdl(Source, mdl, MmGetMdlVirtualAddress(Source), 0);
  1859	        *Target = mdl;
  1860	        Target = &mdl->Next;
  1861	        Source = Source->Next;
  1862	    }
  1863	    return STATUS_SUCCESS;
  1864	}
  1865	
  1866	static VOID StageReleasePagingIo(PSTAGE_PAGING_IO Io)
  1867	{
  1868	    if (InterlockedDecrement(&Io->References) == 0)
  1869	        ExFreePoolWithTag(Io, STAGE_TAG);
  1870	}
  1871	
  1872	static VOID StagePagingComplete(PFLT_CALLBACK_DATA Data, PVOID Context)
  1873	{
  1874	    PSTAGE_PAGING_IO io = Context;
  1875	    PFLT_CALLBACK_DATA original = io->Original;
  1876	    original->IoStatus = Data->IoStatus;
  1877	    /* The child owns partial MDLs over the original request's still-locked
  1878	     * pages. FltFreeCallbackData frees its MDL chain, never the upper MDLs. */
  1879	    FltFreeCallbackData(Data);
  1880	    if (InterlockedCompareExchange(&io->State, 2, 0) == 1) {
  1881	        ExReleaseRundownProtection(&io->Stream->PagingRundown);
  1882	        FltCompletePendedPreOperation(original, FLT_PREOP_COMPLETE, NULL);
  1883	#if SAFEUPLOAD_STAGING_PROTOTYPE
  1884	        SafeUploadPolicyAdmissionRelease((PSAFEUPLOAD_ADMISSION_EPOCH_TOKEN)io->AdmissionToken);
  1885	        io->AdmissionToken = NULL;
  1886	#endif
  1887	    }
  1888	    StageReleasePagingIo(io);
  1889	}
  1890	
  1891	static FLT_PREOP_CALLBACK_STATUS StageRoutePaging(PFLT_CALLBACK_DATA Data,
  1892	    PSTAGE_STREAM Stream, PVOID AdmissionToken)
  1893	{
  1894	    PSTAGE_PAGING_IO io;
  1895	    PFLT_CALLBACK_DATA child = NULL;
  1896	    NTSTATUS status;
  1897	    FLT_PREOP_CALLBACK_STATUS result;
  1898	    if ((Stream->ReadOnly && Data->Iopb->MajorFunction != IRP_MJ_READ) ||
  1899	        !ExAcquireRundownProtection(&Stream->PagingRundown)) {
  1900	        Data->IoStatus.Status = STATUS_MEDIA_WRITE_PROTECTED;
  1901	        Data->IoStatus.Information = 0;
  1902	        return FLT_PREOP_COMPLETE;
  1903	    }
  1904	    /* Do not retarget the upper IRP: its actual remaining stack locations can
  1905	     * be smaller than the backing device's stack even after the CREATE guard.
  1906	     * The generic allocator is needed here for APC-level paging and Cc's
  1907	     * AdvanceOnly SET_INFORMATION, preserving the original operation/MDL. */
  1908	    if (KeGetCurrentIrql() > APC_LEVEL ||
  1909	        (!FlagOn(Data->Iopb->IrpFlags, IRP_PAGING_IO) && KeGetCurrentIrql() != PASSIVE_LEVEL)) {
  1910	        status = STATUS_INVALID_DEVICE_STATE;
  1911	        goto Fail;
  1912	    }
  1913	    if (FltIsIoCanceled(Data)) { status = STATUS_CANCELLED; goto Fail; }
  1914	    io = ExAllocatePool2(POOL_FLAG_NON_PAGED, sizeof(*io), STAGE_TAG);
  1915	    if (io == NULL) { status = STATUS_INSUFFICIENT_RESOURCES; goto Fail; }
  1916	    status = FltAllocateCallbackDataEx(Stream->BackingInstance, Stream->BackingObject,
  1917	        FLT_ALLOCATE_CALLBACK_DATA_PREALLOCATE_ALL_MEMORY, &child);
  1918	    if (!NT_SUCCESS(status)) { ExFreePoolWithTag(io, STAGE_TAG); goto Fail; }
  1919	    child->Iopb->MajorFunction = Data->Iopb->MajorFunction;
  1920	    child->Iopb->MinorFunction = Data->Iopb->MinorFunction;
  1921	    child->Iopb->OperationFlags = Data->Iopb->OperationFlags;
  1922	    child->Iopb->Parameters = Data->Iopb->Parameters;
  1923	    if (Data->Iopb->MajorFunction == IRP_MJ_READ || Data->Iopb->MajorFunction == IRP_MJ_WRITE) {
  1924	        PMDL sourceMdl = Data->Iopb->MajorFunction == IRP_MJ_READ ?
  1925	            Data->Iopb->Parameters.Read.MdlAddress : Data->Iopb->Parameters.Write.MdlAddress;
  1926	        PMDL *targetMdl = Data->Iopb->MajorFunction == IRP_MJ_READ ?
  1927	            &child->Iopb->Parameters.Read.MdlAddress : &child->Iopb->Parameters.Write.MdlAddress;
  1928	        status = StageClonePagingMdls(sourceMdl, targetMdl);
  1929	        if (!NT_SUCCESS(status)) {
  1930	            FltFreeCallbackData(child);
  1931	            ExFreePoolWithTag(io, STAGE_TAG);
  1932	            goto Fail;
  1933	        }
  1934	    }
  1935	    /* Buffer ownership, allocation and completion flags belong to the newly
  1936	     * generated IRP. Only the original I/O semantics are transferable. */
  1937	    child->Iopb->IrpFlags = Data->Iopb->IrpFlags &
  1938	        (IRP_NOCACHE | IRP_PAGING_IO | IRP_SYNCHRONOUS_PAGING_IO);
  1939	    child->Iopb->TargetInstance = Stream->BackingInstance;
  1940	    child->Iopb->TargetFileObject = Stream->BackingObject;
  1941	    io->Stream = Stream;
  1942	    io->Original = Data;
  1943	    io->AdmissionToken = AdmissionToken;
  1944	    io->References = 2;
  1945	    io->State = 0;
  1946	    /* FltPerformAsynchronousIo always invokes completion, even on failure;
  1947	     * completion may run before this call returns. Both paths complete the
  1948	     * upper operation exactly once and release rundown only after lower I/O. */
  1949	    (VOID)FltPerformAsynchronousIo(child, StagePagingComplete, io);
  1950	    if (InterlockedCompareExchange(&io->State, 1, 0) == 2) {
  1951	        ExReleaseRundownProtection(&Stream->PagingRundown);
  1952	        result = FLT_PREOP_COMPLETE;
  1953	    } else result = FLT_PREOP_PENDING;
  1954	    StageReleasePagingIo(io);
  1955	    return result;
  1956	Fail:
  1957	    ExReleaseRundownProtection(&Stream->PagingRundown);
  1958	    Data->IoStatus.Status = status;
  1959	    Data->IoStatus.Information = 0;
  1960	    return FLT_PREOP_COMPLETE;
  1961	}
  1962	
  1963	
  1964	static FLT_PREOP_CALLBACK_STATUS StagePreOperation(PFLT_CALLBACK_DATA Data,
  1965	    PCFLT_RELATED_OBJECTS Objects, PVOID *CompletionContext, PVOID AdmissionToken)
  1966	{
  1967	    PSTAGE_STREAM stream;
  1968	    PSTAGE_HANDLE handle;
  1969	    PFILE_OBJECT file = Data->Iopb->TargetFileObject;
  1970	    NTSTATUS status = STATUS_NOT_SUPPORTED;
  1971	    UNREFERENCED_PARAMETER(Objects);
  1972	    *CompletionContext = NULL;
  1973	    stream = StageStreamForObject(file);
  1974	    if (stream == NULL) return FLT_PREOP_SUCCESS_NO_CALLBACK;
  1975	    if (Data->Iopb->MajorFunction == IRP_MJ_QUERY_OPEN) return FLT_PREOP_DISALLOW_FSFILTER_IO;
  1976	    if (FLT_IS_FASTIO_OPERATION(Data)) return FLT_PREOP_DISALLOW_FASTIO;
  1977	    Data->IoStatus.Information = 0;
  1978	    handle = file->FsContext2;
  1979	    switch (Data->Iopb->MajorFunction) {
  1980	    case IRP_MJ_LOCK_CONTROL:
  1981	        if (handle == NULL || handle->Cleaned) { status = STATUS_FILE_CLOSED; break; }
  1982	        {
  1983	            PEPROCESS process = FltGetRequestorProcess(Data);
  1984	            ULONG index;
  1985	            FLT_PREOP_CALLBACK_STATUS lockResult;
  1986	            if (process == NULL) { status = STATUS_ACCESS_DENIED; break; }
  1987	            StageAcquire(&stream->Resource);
  1988	            for (index = 0; index < handle->LockOwnerCount; ++index)
  1989	                if (handle->LockOwners[index] == process) break;
  1990	            if (index == handle->LockOwnerCount) {
  1991	                if (index == RTL_NUMBER_OF(handle->LockOwners)) {
  1992	                    StageRelease(&stream->Resource);
  1993	                    status = STATUS_INSUFFICIENT_RESOURCES; break;
  1994	                }
  1995	                ObReferenceObject(process);
  1996	                handle->LockOwners[handle->LockOwnerCount++] = process;
  1997	            }
  1998	            /* FltMgr owns waiting, cancellation and completion. The request's
  1999	             * file object pins this version until a pending lock completes. */
  2000	            lockResult = FltProcessFileLock(stream->ByteLocks, Data, NULL);
  2001	            StageRelease(&stream->Resource);
  2002	            return lockResult;
  2003	        }
  2004	    case IRP_MJ_READ:
  2005	    case IRP_MJ_WRITE:
  2006	        if (FlagOn(Data->Iopb->IrpFlags, IRP_PAGING_IO)) {
  2007	            /* The upper cache/section and original request stay on the
  2008	             * destination. A separate backing I/O owns paging rundown. */
  2009	            return StageRoutePaging(Data, stream, AdmissionToken);
  2010	        }
  2011	        if (handle == NULL || handle->Cleaned) {
  2012	            status = STATUS_FILE_CLOSED;
  2013	            break;
  2014	        }
  2015	        status = StageReadWrite(Data, stream);
  2016	        break;
  2017	    case IRP_MJ_QUERY_INFORMATION:
  2018	        status = StageQuery(Data, stream, handle);
  2019	        break;
  2020	    case IRP_MJ_QUERY_VOLUME_INFORMATION:
  2021	        status = FltQueryVolumeInformation(stream->OriginalInstance, &Data->IoStatus,
  2022	            Data->Iopb->Parameters.QueryVolumeInformation.VolumeBuffer,
  2023	            Data->Iopb->Parameters.QueryVolumeInformation.Length,
  2024	            Data->Iopb->Parameters.QueryVolumeInformation.FsInformationClass);
  2025	        break;
  2026	    case IRP_MJ_SET_INFORMATION:
  2027	        if (Data->Iopb->Parameters.SetFileInformation.FileInformationClass == FileEndOfFileInformation &&
  2028	            Data->Iopb->Parameters.SetFileInformation.AdvanceOnly) {
  2029	            /* Cc may advance the on-disk VDL after cleanup. This is not a
  2030	             * resize and must never reinitialize the upper private cache map. */
  2031	            return StageRoutePaging(Data, stream, AdmissionToken);
  2032	        }
  2033	        if (handle == NULL || handle->Cleaned) {
  2034	            status = STATUS_FILE_CLOSED;
  2035	            break;
  2036	        }
  2037	        if (Data->Iopb->Parameters.SetFileInformation.FileInformationClass == FileRenameInformation ||
  2038	            Data->Iopb->Parameters.SetFileInformation.FileInformationClass == FileRenameInformationEx)
  2039	            status = StageRename(Data, stream);
  2040	        else if ((stream->ReadOnly || stream->RenameExchange != NULL) &&
  2041	            Data->Iopb->Parameters.SetFileInformation.FileInformationClass != FilePositionInformation) {
  2042	            status = STATUS_ACCESS_DENIED;
  2043	        }
  2044	        else if (Data->Iopb->Parameters.SetFileInformation.FileInformationClass == FileEndOfFileInformation &&
  2045	            Data->Iopb->Parameters.SetFileInformation.Length >= sizeof(FILE_END_OF_FILE_INFORMATION)) {
  2046	            StageAcquire(&stream->Resource);
  2047	            __try {
  2048	                status = StageResize(stream, file,
  2049	                    ((PFILE_END_OF_FILE_INFORMATION)Data->Iopb->Parameters.SetFileInformation.InfoBuffer)->EndOfFile);
  2050	            } __except(EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER) { status = GetExceptionCode(); }
  2051	            StageRelease(&stream->Resource);
  2052	        } else if (Data->Iopb->Parameters.SetFileInformation.FileInformationClass == FilePositionInformation &&
  2053	            Data->Iopb->Parameters.SetFileInformation.Length >= sizeof(FILE_POSITION_INFORMATION)) {
  2054	            LARGE_INTEGER position = ((PFILE_POSITION_INFORMATION)Data->Iopb->Parameters.SetFileInformation.InfoBuffer)->CurrentByteOffset;
  2055	            if (position.QuadPart >= 0) { file->CurrentByteOffset = position; status = STATUS_SUCCESS; }
  2056	            else status = STATUS_INVALID_PARAMETER;
  2057	        } else if (Data->Iopb->Parameters.SetFileInformation.FileInformationClass == FileAllocationInformation &&
  2058	            Data->Iopb->Parameters.SetFileInformation.Length >= sizeof(FILE_ALLOCATION_INFORMATION)) {
  2059	            PFILE_ALLOCATION_INFORMATION allocation = Data->Iopb->Parameters.SetFileInformation.InfoBuffer;
  2060	            FILE_STANDARD_INFORMATION standard = {0};
  2061	            StageAcquire(&stream->Resource);
  2062	            __try {
  2063	                if (stream->ReadOnly || stream->RenameExchange != NULL)
  2064	                    status = STATUS_ACCESS_DENIED;
  2065	                else if (allocation->AllocationSize.QuadPart < 0 || allocation->AllocationSize.QuadPart > STAGE_MAX_BYTES)
  2066	                    status = STATUS_FILE_TOO_LARGE;
  2067	                else {
  2068	                    status = STATUS_SUCCESS;
  2069	                    if (allocation->AllocationSize.QuadPart < stream->Header.FileSize.QuadPart)
  2070	                        status = StageResize(stream, file, allocation->AllocationSize);
  2071	                    if (NT_SUCCESS(status)) status = FltSetInformationFile(stream->BackingInstance,
  2072	                        stream->BackingObject, allocation, sizeof(*allocation), FileAllocationInformation);
  2073	                    if (NT_SUCCESS(status)) status = FltQueryInformationFile(stream->BackingInstance,
  2074	                        stream->BackingObject, &standard, sizeof(standard), FileStandardInformation, NULL);
  2075	                    if (NT_SUCCESS(status)) {
  2076	                        stream->Header.AllocationSize = standard.AllocationSize;
  2077	                        if (stream->Sections.SharedCacheMap != NULL) {
  2078	                            CC_FILE_SIZES sizes;
  2079	                            StageInitializeCache(stream, file);
  2080	                            sizes.AllocationSize = standard.AllocationSize;

   116	static LONG StageAdmissionTraceNextState(_In_ LONG CurrentState, _In_ BOOLEAN Enable)
   117	{
   118	    ULONG nextState = (((ULONG)CurrentState & 0x7ffffffeUL) + 2UL) & 0x7ffffffeUL;
   119	    if (Enable) nextState |= 1UL;
   120	    return (LONG)nextState;
   121	}

   123	static VOID StageAdmissionTraceReset(VOID)
   124	{
   125	    ULONG index;
   126	    InterlockedExchange64(&AdmissionTraceNextSequence, 0);
   127	    for (index = 0; index < SAFEUPLOAD_ADMISSION_TRACE_RING_ENTRIES; index += 1) {
   128	        InterlockedExchange64((volatile LONG64 *)&AdmissionTraceRing[index].Sequence, 0);
   129	    }
   130	    InterlockedExchange(&AdmissionTraceWriterBusy, 0);
   131	    RtlZeroMemory(&AdmissionTraceCounters, sizeof(AdmissionTraceCounters));
   132	    for (index = 0; index < STAGE_TRACE_WATCH_SLOTS; index += 1) {
   133	        InterlockedExchangePointer((PVOID volatile *)&AdmissionTraceWatch[index], NULL);
   134	    }
   135	}

   156	BOOLEAN SafeUploadStageAdmissionTraceBegin(_In_ LONG TraceState)
   157	{
   158	    if ((TraceState & 1) == 0 || !ExAcquireRundownProtection(&AdmissionTraceRundown)) {
   159	        return FALSE;
   160	    }
   161	
   162	    KeMemoryBarrier();
   163	    if (SafeUploadAdmissionTraceControlState != TraceState) {
   164	        ExReleaseRundownProtection(&AdmissionTraceRundown);
   165	        return FALSE;
   166	    }
   167	
   168	    return TRUE;
   169	}
   170	
   171	VOID SafeUploadStageAdmissionTraceEnd(VOID)
   172	{
   173	    ExReleaseRundownProtection(&AdmissionTraceRundown);
   174	}

  1039	static BOOLEAN StageCacheAcquire(PVOID Context, BOOLEAN Wait)
  1040	{
  1041	    PSTAGE_STREAM stream = Context;
  1042	    KeEnterCriticalRegion();
  1043	    if (!ExAcquireResourceSharedLite(&stream->PagingResource, Wait)) {
  1044	        KeLeaveCriticalRegion();
  1045	        return FALSE;
  1046	    }
  1047	    return TRUE;
  1048	}

  1050	static VOID StageCacheRelease(PVOID Context)
  1051	{
  1052	    PSTAGE_STREAM stream = Context;
  1053	    StageRelease(&stream->PagingResource);
  1054	}

  1060	static VOID StageInitializeCache(PSTAGE_STREAM Stream, PFILE_OBJECT FileObject)

  1066	    sizes.ValidDataLength = Stream->Header.ValidDataLength;

  1068	}

  1072	    IO_STATUS_BLOCK io = {0};
  1073	    if (Stream->Sections.DataSectionObject != NULL) {
  1074	        CcFlushCache(&Stream->Sections, NULL, 0, &io);
  1075	        if (!NT_SUCCESS(io.Status)) return io.Status;
  1076	    }
  1077	    return STATUS_SUCCESS;
  1078	}
  1079	
  1080	static NTSTATUS StageFlush(PSTAGE_STREAM Stream)
  1081	{
  1082	    NTSTATUS status = StageFlushCache(Stream);
  1083	    if (!NT_SUCCESS(status)) return status;
  1084	    return Stream->ReadOnly ? STATUS_SUCCESS : FltFlushBuffers(Stream->BackingInstance, Stream->BackingObject);
  1085	}
  1086	
  1087	static VOID StageFreeStream(PSTAGE_STREAM Stream)
  1088	{
  1089	    if (Stream->BackingObject != NULL) ObDereferenceObject(Stream->BackingObject);
  1090	    if (Stream->BackingHandle != NULL) FltClose(Stream->BackingHandle);
  1091	    if (Stream->BackingInstance != NULL) FltObjectDereference(Stream->BackingInstance);
  1092	    if (Stream->OriginalInstance != NULL) FltObjectDereference(Stream->OriginalInstance);
  1093	    if (Stream->RenameExchange != NULL) ExFreePoolWithTag(Stream->RenameExchange, STAGE_TAG);
  1094	    if (Stream->ByteLocks != NULL) FltFreeFileLock(Stream->ByteLocks);
  1095	    if (Stream->PagingInitialized) ExDeleteResourceLite(&Stream->PagingResource);
  1096	    if (Stream->ResourceInitialized) ExDeleteResourceLite(&Stream->Resource);
  1097	    ExFreePoolWithTag(Stream, STAGE_TAG);
  1098	}

  1104	{

  1438	    if (NT_SUCCESS(status) && !newStream && Writer &&

  1445	        __except(EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER) { status = GetExceptionCode(); }

  1461	        Data->Iopb->Parameters.Create.SecurityContext->AccessState->RemainingDesiredAccess &= ~(granted | MAXIMUM_ALLOWED);
  1462	        information = !exists ? FILE_CREATED : disposition == FILE_SUPERSEDE ? FILE_SUPERSEDED :
  1463	            (disposition == FILE_OVERWRITE || disposition == FILE_OVERWRITE_IF) ? FILE_OVERWRITTEN : FILE_OPENED;

  1482	    }
  1483	    StageRelease(&StageNamespaceResource);

  1485	}
