  1487	
  1488	/* Materialize the extended range with ordinary noncached backing writes.
  1489	 * Paging writes do not advance NTFS's valid data length. Claiming a larger
  1490	 * upper VDL without zeroing could expose old disk contents when Cc advances it.
  1491	 * Preserve an existing partial sector; the backing has no second data cache. */
  1492	static NTSTATUS StageZeroGrowth(PSTAGE_STREAM Stream, LARGE_INTEGER Size)
  1493	{
  1494	    PVOID buffer;
  1495	    LARGE_INTEGER offset;
  1496	    LONGLONG rounded = (Size.QuadPart + Stream->SectorSize - 1) & ~((LONGLONG)Stream->SectorSize - 1);
  1497	    ULONG length, transferred, tail;
  1498	    NTSTATUS status;
  1499	    if (Size.QuadPart <= Stream->Header.FileSize.QuadPart) return STATUS_SUCCESS;
  1500	    /* Preserve existing upper dirty bytes before reading the partial tail.
  1501	     * The noncached lower object has no data cache to drain. Device durability
  1502	     * belongs to explicit flush/cleanup/seal, not a resize performed by Mm
  1503	     * while creating an extending section. */
  1504	    status = StageFlushCache(Stream);
  1505	    if (!NT_SUCCESS(status)) return status;
  1506	    buffer = FltAllocatePoolAlignedWithTag(Stream->BackingInstance, NonPagedPoolNx, 65536, STAGE_TAG);
  1507	    if (buffer == NULL) return STATUS_INSUFFICIENT_RESOURCES;
  1508	    RtlZeroMemory(buffer, 65536);
  1509	    offset.QuadPart = Stream->Header.FileSize.QuadPart & ~((LONGLONG)Stream->SectorSize - 1);
  1510	    tail = (ULONG)(Stream->Header.FileSize.QuadPart - offset.QuadPart);
  1511	    if (tail != 0) {
  1512	        status = FltReadFile(Stream->BackingInstance, Stream->BackingObject, &offset,
  1513	            Stream->SectorSize, buffer, FLTFL_IO_OPERATION_NON_CACHED |
  1514	            FLTFL_IO_OPERATION_DO_NOT_UPDATE_BYTE_OFFSET, &transferred, NULL, NULL);
  1515	        if (!NT_SUCCESS(status) || transferred < tail) {
  1516	            if (NT_SUCCESS(status)) status = STATUS_UNEXPECTED_IO_ERROR;
  1517	            goto Exit;
  1518	        }
  1519	        RtlZeroMemory((PUCHAR)buffer + tail, 65536 - tail);
  1520	    }
  1521	    while (offset.QuadPart < rounded) {
  1522	        length = (ULONG)min(65536LL, rounded - offset.QuadPart);
  1523	        status = FltWriteFile(Stream->BackingInstance, Stream->BackingObject, &offset,
  1524	            length, buffer, FLTFL_IO_OPERATION_NON_CACHED |
  1525	            FLTFL_IO_OPERATION_DO_NOT_UPDATE_BYTE_OFFSET, &transferred, NULL, NULL);
  1526	        if (!NT_SUCCESS(status) || transferred != length) {
  1527	            if (NT_SUCCESS(status)) status = STATUS_UNEXPECTED_IO_ERROR;
  1528	            goto Exit;
  1529	        }
  1530	        offset.QuadPart += length;
  1531	        if (tail != 0) { RtlZeroMemory(buffer, 65536); tail = 0; }
  1532	    }
  1533	Exit:
  1534	    FltFreePoolAlignedWithTag(Stream->BackingInstance, buffer, STAGE_TAG);
  1535	    return status;
  1536	}
  1537	
  1538	static NTSTATUS StageResize(PSTAGE_STREAM Stream, PFILE_OBJECT FileObject, LARGE_INTEGER Size)
  1539	{
  1540	    FILE_END_OF_FILE_INFORMATION end;
  1541	    CC_FILE_SIZES sizes;
  1542	    NTSTATUS status;
  1543	    NT_ASSERT(ExIsResourceAcquiredExclusiveLite(&Stream->Resource));
  1544	    if (Stream->ReadOnly || Stream->RenameExchange != NULL) return STATUS_ACCESS_DENIED;
  1545	    if (Size.QuadPart < 0 || Size.QuadPart > STAGE_MAX_BYTES) return STATUS_FILE_TOO_LARGE;
  1546	    if (Size.QuadPart < Stream->Header.FileSize.QuadPart &&
  1547	        !MmCanFileBeTruncated(&Stream->Sections, &Size)) return STATUS_USER_MAPPED_FILE;
  1548	    status = StageZeroGrowth(Stream, Size);
  1549	    if (!NT_SUCCESS(status)) return status;
  1550	    end.EndOfFile = Size;
  1551	    status = FltSetInformationFile(Stream->BackingInstance, Stream->BackingObject,
  1552	        &end, sizeof(end), FileEndOfFileInformation);
  1553	    if (!NT_SUCCESS(status)) return status;
  1554	    Stream->Header.FileSize = Size;
  1555	    Stream->Header.AllocationSize.QuadPart = (Size.QuadPart + PAGE_SIZE - 1) & ~((LONGLONG)PAGE_SIZE - 1);
  1556	    Stream->Header.ValidDataLength = Size; /* Every extended byte was materialized. */
  1557	    if (Stream->Sections.SharedCacheMap != NULL) {
  1558	        StageInitializeCache(Stream, FileObject);
  1559	        sizes.AllocationSize = Stream->Header.AllocationSize;
  1560	        sizes.FileSize = Size;
  1561	        sizes.ValidDataLength = Size;
  1562	        CcSetFileSizes(FileObject, &sizes);
  1563	    }
  1564	    return STATUS_SUCCESS;
  1565	}

  1567	static NTSTATUS StageReadWrite(PFLT_CALLBACK_DATA Data, PSTAGE_STREAM Stream)
  1568	{
  1569	    BOOLEAN write = Data->Iopb->MajorFunction == IRP_MJ_WRITE;
  1570	    ULONG length = write ? Data->Iopb->Parameters.Write.Length : Data->Iopb->Parameters.Read.Length;
  1571	    LARGE_INTEGER offset = write ? Data->Iopb->Parameters.Write.ByteOffset : Data->Iopb->Parameters.Read.ByteOffset;
  1572	    PFILE_OBJECT file = Data->Iopb->TargetFileObject;
  1573	    PMDL mdl = write ? Data->Iopb->Parameters.Write.MdlAddress : Data->Iopb->Parameters.Read.MdlAddress;
  1574	    PVOID buffer;
  1575	    NTSTATUS status;
  1576	    if (write && (Stream->ReadOnly || Stream->RenameExchange != NULL)) return STATUS_ACCESS_DENIED;
  1577	    if (length == 0) return STATUS_SUCCESS;
  1578	    if (mdl == NULL && !FLT_IS_SYSTEM_BUFFER(Data)) {
  1579	        status = FltLockUserBuffer(Data);
  1580	        if (!NT_SUCCESS(status)) return status;
  1581	        mdl = write ? Data->Iopb->Parameters.Write.MdlAddress : Data->Iopb->Parameters.Read.MdlAddress;
  1582	    }
  1583	    buffer = mdl != NULL ? MmGetSystemAddressForMdlSafe(mdl, NormalPagePriority | MdlMappingNoExecute) :
  1584	        write ? Data->Iopb->Parameters.Write.WriteBuffer : Data->Iopb->Parameters.Read.ReadBuffer;
  1585	    if (buffer == NULL) return STATUS_INSUFFICIENT_RESOURCES;
  1586	    StageAcquire(&Stream->Resource);
  1587	    __try {
  1588	        LARGE_INTEGER lockLength;
  1589	        /* Rename publishes its freeze while holding this same resource.
  1590	         * The early check can become stale while locking the user's buffer. */
  1591	        if (write && (Stream->ReadOnly || Stream->RenameExchange != NULL)) {
  1592	            status = STATUS_ACCESS_DENIED;
  1593	            __leave;
  1594	        }
  1595	        if (write && (offset.QuadPart == (LONGLONG)(LONG)FILE_WRITE_TO_END_OF_FILE ||
  1596	            !FlagOn(((PSTAGE_HANDLE)file->FsContext2)->GrantedAccess, FILE_WRITE_DATA))) offset = Stream->Header.FileSize;
  1597	        if (offset.QuadPart == (LONGLONG)(LONG)FILE_USE_FILE_POINTER_POSITION) offset = file->CurrentByteOffset;
  1598	        if (offset.QuadPart < 0 || offset.QuadPart > STAGE_MAX_BYTES ||
  1599	            length > STAGE_MAX_BYTES - (ULONGLONG)offset.QuadPart) {
  1600	            status = STATUS_INVALID_PARAMETER;
  1601	            __leave;
  1602	        }
  1603	        lockLength.QuadPart = length;
  1604	        if (write ? !FsRtlFastCheckLockForWrite(Stream->ByteLocks, &offset, &lockLength,
  1605	                Data->Iopb->Parameters.Write.Key, file, FltGetRequestorProcess(Data)) :
  1606	            !FsRtlFastCheckLockForRead(Stream->ByteLocks, &offset, &lockLength,
  1607	                Data->Iopb->Parameters.Read.Key, file, FltGetRequestorProcess(Data))) {
  1608	            status = STATUS_FILE_LOCK_CONFLICT;
  1609	            __leave;
  1610	        }
  1611	        if (!write) {
  1612	            if (offset.QuadPart >= Stream->Header.FileSize.QuadPart) {
  1613	                status = STATUS_END_OF_FILE;
  1614	                __leave;
  1615	            }

  1851	static NTSTATUS StageClonePagingMdls(PMDL Source, PMDL *Target)
  1852	{
  1853	    *Target = NULL;
  1854	    while (Source != NULL) {
  1855	        PMDL mdl = IoAllocateMdl(MmGetMdlVirtualAddress(Source),
  1856	            MmGetMdlByteCount(Source), FALSE, FALSE, NULL);
  1857	        if (mdl == NULL) return STATUS_INSUFFICIENT_RESOURCES;
  1858	        IoBuildPartialMdl(Source, mdl, MmGetMdlVirtualAddress(Source), 0);
  1859	        *Target = mdl;
  1860	        Target = &mdl->Next;
  1861	        Source = Source->Next;
  1862	    }
  1863	    return STATUS_SUCCESS;
  1864	}

  1866	static VOID StageReleasePagingIo(PSTAGE_PAGING_IO Io)
  1867	{
  1868	    if (InterlockedDecrement(&Io->References) == 0)
  1869	        ExFreePoolWithTag(Io, STAGE_TAG);
  1870	}
  1871	
  1872	static VOID StagePagingComplete(PFLT_CALLBACK_DATA Data, PVOID Context)
  1873	{
  1874	    PSTAGE_PAGING_IO io = Context;
  1875	    PFLT_CALLBACK_DATA original = io->Original;
  1876	    original->IoStatus = Data->IoStatus;
  1877	    /* The child owns partial MDLs over the original request's still-locked
  1878	     * pages. FltFreeCallbackData frees its MDL chain, never the upper MDLs. */
  1879	    FltFreeCallbackData(Data);
  1880	    if (InterlockedCompareExchange(&io->State, 2, 0) == 1) {
  1881	        ExReleaseRundownProtection(&io->Stream->PagingRundown);
  1882	        FltCompletePendedPreOperation(original, FLT_PREOP_COMPLETE, NULL);
  1883	#if SAFEUPLOAD_STAGING_PROTOTYPE
  1884	        SafeUploadPolicyAdmissionRelease((PSAFEUPLOAD_ADMISSION_EPOCH_TOKEN)io->AdmissionToken);
  1885	        io->AdmissionToken = NULL;
  1886	#endif
  1887	    }
  1888	    StageReleasePagingIo(io);
  1889	}

  1891	static FLT_PREOP_CALLBACK_STATUS StageRoutePaging(PFLT_CALLBACK_DATA Data,
  1892	    PSTAGE_STREAM Stream, PVOID AdmissionToken)
  1893	{
  1894	    PSTAGE_PAGING_IO io;
  1895	    PFLT_CALLBACK_DATA child = NULL;
  1896	    NTSTATUS status;
  1897	    FLT_PREOP_CALLBACK_STATUS result;
  1898	    if ((Stream->ReadOnly && Data->Iopb->MajorFunction != IRP_MJ_READ) ||
  1899	        !ExAcquireRundownProtection(&Stream->PagingRundown)) {
  1900	        Data->IoStatus.Status = STATUS_MEDIA_WRITE_PROTECTED;
  1901	        Data->IoStatus.Information = 0;
  1902	        return FLT_PREOP_COMPLETE;
  1903	    }
  1904	    /* Do not retarget the upper IRP: its actual remaining stack locations can
  1905	     * be smaller than the backing device's stack even after the CREATE guard.
  1906	     * The generic allocator is needed here for APC-level paging and Cc's
  1907	     * AdvanceOnly SET_INFORMATION, preserving the original operation/MDL. */
  1908	    if (KeGetCurrentIrql() > APC_LEVEL ||
  1909	        (!FlagOn(Data->Iopb->IrpFlags, IRP_PAGING_IO) && KeGetCurrentIrql() != PASSIVE_LEVEL)) {
  1910	        status = STATUS_INVALID_DEVICE_STATE;
  1911	        goto Fail;
  1912	    }
  1913	    if (FltIsIoCanceled(Data)) { status = STATUS_CANCELLED; goto Fail; }
  1914	    io = ExAllocatePool2(POOL_FLAG_NON_PAGED, sizeof(*io), STAGE_TAG);
  1915	    if (io == NULL) { status = STATUS_INSUFFICIENT_RESOURCES; goto Fail; }
  1916	    status = FltAllocateCallbackDataEx(Stream->BackingInstance, Stream->BackingObject,
  1917	        FLT_ALLOCATE_CALLBACK_DATA_PREALLOCATE_ALL_MEMORY, &child);
  1918	    if (!NT_SUCCESS(status)) { ExFreePoolWithTag(io, STAGE_TAG); goto Fail; }
  1919	    child->Iopb->MajorFunction = Data->Iopb->MajorFunction;
  1920	    child->Iopb->MinorFunction = Data->Iopb->MinorFunction;
  1921	    child->Iopb->OperationFlags = Data->Iopb->OperationFlags;
  1922	    child->Iopb->Parameters = Data->Iopb->Parameters;
  1923	    if (Data->Iopb->MajorFunction == IRP_MJ_READ || Data->Iopb->MajorFunction == IRP_MJ_WRITE) {
  1924	        PMDL sourceMdl = Data->Iopb->MajorFunction == IRP_MJ_READ ?
  1925	            Data->Iopb->Parameters.Read.MdlAddress : Data->Iopb->Parameters.Write.MdlAddress;
  1926	        PMDL *targetMdl = Data->Iopb->MajorFunction == IRP_MJ_READ ?
  1927	            &child->Iopb->Parameters.Read.MdlAddress : &child->Iopb->Parameters.Write.MdlAddress;
  1928	        status = StageClonePagingMdls(sourceMdl, targetMdl);
  1929	        if (!NT_SUCCESS(status)) {
  1930	            FltFreeCallbackData(child);
  1931	            ExFreePoolWithTag(io, STAGE_TAG);
  1932	            goto Fail;
  1933	        }
  1934	    }
  1935	    /* Buffer ownership, allocation and completion flags belong to the newly
  1936	     * generated IRP. Only the original I/O semantics are transferable. */
  1937	    child->Iopb->IrpFlags = Data->Iopb->IrpFlags &
  1938	        (IRP_NOCACHE | IRP_PAGING_IO | IRP_SYNCHRONOUS_PAGING_IO);
  1939	    child->Iopb->TargetInstance = Stream->BackingInstance;
  1940	    child->Iopb->TargetFileObject = Stream->BackingObject;
  1941	    io->Stream = Stream;
  1942	    io->Original = Data;
  1943	    io->AdmissionToken = AdmissionToken;
  1944	    io->References = 2;
  1945	    io->State = 0;
  1946	    /* FltPerformAsynchronousIo always invokes completion, even on failure;
  1947	     * completion may run before this call returns. Both paths complete the
  1948	     * upper operation exactly once and release rundown only after lower I/O. */
  1949	    (VOID)FltPerformAsynchronousIo(child, StagePagingComplete, io);
  1950	    if (InterlockedCompareExchange(&io->State, 1, 0) == 2) {
  1951	        ExReleaseRundownProtection(&Stream->PagingRundown);
  1952	        result = FLT_PREOP_COMPLETE;
  1953	    } else result = FLT_PREOP_PENDING;
  1954	    StageReleasePagingIo(io);
  1955	    return result;
  1956	Fail:
  1957	    ExReleaseRundownProtection(&Stream->PagingRundown);
  1958	    Data->IoStatus.Status = status;
  1959	    Data->IoStatus.Information = 0;
  1960	    return FLT_PREOP_COMPLETE;
  1961	}
  1962	
  1963	
  1964	static FLT_PREOP_CALLBACK_STATUS StagePreOperation(PFLT_CALLBACK_DATA Data,
  1965	    PCFLT_RELATED_OBJECTS Objects, PVOID *CompletionContext, PVOID AdmissionToken)
  1966	{
  1967	    PSTAGE_STREAM stream;
  1968	    PSTAGE_HANDLE handle;
  1969	    PFILE_OBJECT file = Data->Iopb->TargetFileObject;
  1970	    NTSTATUS status = STATUS_NOT_SUPPORTED;
  1971	    UNREFERENCED_PARAMETER(Objects);
  1972	    *CompletionContext = NULL;
  1973	    stream = StageStreamForObject(file);
  1974	    if (stream == NULL) return FLT_PREOP_SUCCESS_NO_CALLBACK;
  1975	    if (Data->Iopb->MajorFunction == IRP_MJ_QUERY_OPEN) return FLT_PREOP_DISALLOW_FSFILTER_IO;
  1976	    if (FLT_IS_FASTIO_OPERATION(Data)) return FLT_PREOP_DISALLOW_FASTIO;
  1977	    Data->IoStatus.Information = 0;
  1978	    handle = file->FsContext2;
  1979	    switch (Data->Iopb->MajorFunction) {
  1980	    case IRP_MJ_LOCK_CONTROL:
  1981	        if (handle == NULL || handle->Cleaned) { status = STATUS_FILE_CLOSED; break; }
  1982	        {
  1983	            PEPROCESS process = FltGetRequestorProcess(Data);
  1984	            ULONG index;
  1985	            FLT_PREOP_CALLBACK_STATUS lockResult;
  1986	            if (process == NULL) { status = STATUS_ACCESS_DENIED; break; }
  1987	            StageAcquire(&stream->Resource);
  1988	            for (index = 0; index < handle->LockOwnerCount; ++index)
  1989	                if (handle->LockOwners[index] == process) break;
  1990	            if (index == handle->LockOwnerCount) {
  1991	                if (index == RTL_NUMBER_OF(handle->LockOwners)) {
  1992	                    StageRelease(&stream->Resource);
  1993	                    status = STATUS_INSUFFICIENT_RESOURCES; break;
  1994	                }
  1995	                ObReferenceObject(process);
  1996	                handle->LockOwners[handle->LockOwnerCount++] = process;
  1997	            }
  1998	            /* FltMgr owns waiting, cancellation and completion. The request's
  1999	             * file object pins this version until a pending lock completes. */
  2000	            lockResult = FltProcessFileLock(stream->ByteLocks, Data, NULL);
  2001	            StageRelease(&stream->Resource);
  2002	            return lockResult;
  2003	        }
  2004	    case IRP_MJ_READ:
  2005	    case IRP_MJ_WRITE:
  2006	        if (FlagOn(Data->Iopb->IrpFlags, IRP_PAGING_IO)) {
  2007	            /* The upper cache/section and original request stay on the
  2008	             * destination. A separate backing I/O owns paging rundown. */
  2009	            return StageRoutePaging(Data, stream, AdmissionToken);
  2010	        }
  2011	        if (handle == NULL || handle->Cleaned) {
  2012	            status = STATUS_FILE_CLOSED;
  2013	            break;
  2014	        }
  2015	        status = StageReadWrite(Data, stream);
  2016	        break;
  2017	    case IRP_MJ_QUERY_INFORMATION:
  2018	        status = StageQuery(Data, stream, handle);
  2019	        break;
  2020	    case IRP_MJ_QUERY_VOLUME_INFORMATION:
  2021	        status = FltQueryVolumeInformation(stream->OriginalInstance, &Data->IoStatus,
  2022	            Data->Iopb->Parameters.QueryVolumeInformation.VolumeBuffer,
  2023	            Data->Iopb->Parameters.QueryVolumeInformation.Length,
  2024	            Data->Iopb->Parameters.QueryVolumeInformation.FsInformationClass);
  2025	        break;
  2026	    case IRP_MJ_SET_INFORMATION:
  2027	        if (Data->Iopb->Parameters.SetFileInformation.FileInformationClass == FileEndOfFileInformation &&
  2028	            Data->Iopb->Parameters.SetFileInformation.AdvanceOnly) {
  2029	            /* Cc may advance the on-disk VDL after cleanup. This is not a
  2030	             * resize and must never reinitialize the upper private cache map. */
  2031	            return StageRoutePaging(Data, stream, AdmissionToken);
  2032	        }
  2033	        if (handle == NULL || handle->Cleaned) {
  2034	            status = STATUS_FILE_CLOSED;
  2035	            break;
  2036	        }
  2037	        if (Data->Iopb->Parameters.SetFileInformation.FileInformationClass == FileRenameInformation ||
  2038	            Data->Iopb->Parameters.SetFileInformation.FileInformationClass == FileRenameInformationEx)
  2039	            status = StageRename(Data, stream);
  2040	        else if ((stream->ReadOnly || stream->RenameExchange != NULL) &&
  2041	            Data->Iopb->Parameters.SetFileInformation.FileInformationClass != FilePositionInformation) {
  2042	            status = STATUS_ACCESS_DENIED;
  2043	        }
  2044	        else if (Data->Iopb->Parameters.SetFileInformation.FileInformationClass == FileEndOfFileInformation &&
  2045	            Data->Iopb->Parameters.SetFileInformation.Length >= sizeof(FILE_END_OF_FILE_INFORMATION)) {
  2046	            StageAcquire(&stream->Resource);
  2047	            __try {
  2048	                status = StageResize(stream, file,
  2049	                    ((PFILE_END_OF_FILE_INFORMATION)Data->Iopb->Parameters.SetFileInformation.InfoBuffer)->EndOfFile);
  2050	            } __except(EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER) { status = GetExceptionCode(); }
  2051	            StageRelease(&stream->Resource);
  2052	        } else if (Data->Iopb->Parameters.SetFileInformation.FileInformationClass == FilePositionInformation &&
  2053	            Data->Iopb->Parameters.SetFileInformation.Length >= sizeof(FILE_POSITION_INFORMATION)) {
  2054	            LARGE_INTEGER position = ((PFILE_POSITION_INFORMATION)Data->Iopb->Parameters.SetFileInformation.InfoBuffer)->CurrentByteOffset;
  2055	            if (position.QuadPart >= 0) { file->CurrentByteOffset = position; status = STATUS_SUCCESS; }
  2056	            else status = STATUS_INVALID_PARAMETER;
  2057	        } else if (Data->Iopb->Parameters.SetFileInformation.FileInformationClass == FileAllocationInformation &&
  2058	            Data->Iopb->Parameters.SetFileInformation.Length >= sizeof(FILE_ALLOCATION_INFORMATION)) {
  2059	            PFILE_ALLOCATION_INFORMATION allocation = Data->Iopb->Parameters.SetFileInformation.InfoBuffer;
  2060	            FILE_STANDARD_INFORMATION standard = {0};
  2061	            StageAcquire(&stream->Resource);
  2062	            __try {
  2063	                if (stream->ReadOnly || stream->RenameExchange != NULL)
  2064	                    status = STATUS_ACCESS_DENIED;
  2065	                else if (allocation->AllocationSize.QuadPart < 0 || allocation->AllocationSize.QuadPart > STAGE_MAX_BYTES)
  2066	                    status = STATUS_FILE_TOO_LARGE;
  2067	                else {
  2068	                    status = STATUS_SUCCESS;
  2069	                    if (allocation->AllocationSize.QuadPart < stream->Header.FileSize.QuadPart)
  2070	                        status = StageResize(stream, file, allocation->AllocationSize);
  2071	                    if (NT_SUCCESS(status)) status = FltSetInformationFile(stream->BackingInstance,
  2072	                        stream->BackingObject, allocation, sizeof(*allocation), FileAllocationInformation);
  2073	                    if (NT_SUCCESS(status)) status = FltQueryInformationFile(stream->BackingInstance,
  2074	                        stream->BackingObject, &standard, sizeof(standard), FileStandardInformation, NULL);
  2075	                    if (NT_SUCCESS(status)) {
  2076	                        stream->Header.AllocationSize = standard.AllocationSize;
  2077	                        if (stream->Sections.SharedCacheMap != NULL) {
  2078	                            CC_FILE_SIZES sizes;
  2079	                            StageInitializeCache(stream, file);
  2080	                            sizes.AllocationSize = standard.AllocationSize;
  2081	                            sizes.FileSize = stream->Header.FileSize;
  2082	                            sizes.ValidDataLength = stream->Header.ValidDataLength;
  2083	                            CcSetFileSizes(file, &sizes);
  2084	                        }
  2085	                    }
  2086	                }
  2087	            } __except(EXCEPTION_EXECUTE_HANDLER) { status = GetExceptionCode(); }
  2088	            StageRelease(&stream->Resource);
  2089	        }
  2090	        break;
  2091	    case IRP_MJ_FLUSH_BUFFERS:
  2092	        status = StageFlush(stream);
  2093	        break;
  2094	    case IRP_MJ_CLEANUP:
  2095	        if (handle != NULL && !handle->Cleaned) {
  2096	            ULONG index;
  2097	            StageAcquire(&stream->Resource);
  2098	            stream->DrainStatus = StageFlush(stream);
  2099	            IoRemoveShareAccess(file, &stream->ShareAccess);
  2100	            handle->Cleaned = TRUE;
  2101	            /* Cleanup can run in a different process from a lock issued via
  2102	             * a duplicate. Retain and unlock every participating process. */
  2103	            for (index = 0; index < handle->LockOwnerCount; ++index)
  2104	                (VOID)FsRtlFastUnlockAll(stream->ByteLocks, file, handle->LockOwners[index], NULL);
  2105	            SetFlag(file->Flags, FO_CLEANUP_COMPLETE);
  2106	            (VOID)CcUninitializeCacheMap(file, NULL, NULL);
  2107	            StageRelease(&stream->Resource);
  2108	        }
  2109	        status = STATUS_SUCCESS;
  2110	        break;
  2111	    case IRP_MJ_CLOSE:
  2112	        if (handle != NULL) {
  2113	            ULONG index;
  2114	            for (index = 0; index < handle->LockOwnerCount; ++index)
  2115	                ObDereferenceObject(handle->LockOwners[index]);
  2116	            ExFreePoolWithTag(handle, STAGE_TAG);
  2117	            file->FsContext2 = NULL;
  2118	            InterlockedDecrement(&StageFileObjects);
  2119	            InterlockedDecrement(&stream->FileObjects);
  2120	        }
  2121	        status = STATUS_SUCCESS;
  2122	        break;
  2123	    case IRP_MJ_ACQUIRE_FOR_SECTION_SYNCHRONIZATION:
  2124	        StageAcquire(&stream->Resource);
  2125	        if (((stream->ReadOnly || stream->RenameExchange != NULL) ||
  2126	             !SafeUploadIsAuthenticatedClient() ||
  2127	             !SafeUploadInstanceTrustGateSatisfied(stream->OriginalInstance)) &&
  2128	            Data->Iopb->Parameters.AcquireForSectionSynchronization.SyncType == SyncTypeCreateSection &&
  2129	            FlagOn(Data->Iopb->Parameters.AcquireForSectionSynchronization.PageProtection,
  2130	                PAGE_READWRITE | PAGE_EXECUTE_READWRITE)) {
  2131	            StageRelease(&stream->Resource);
  2132	            status = STATUS_ACCESS_DENIED;
  2133	        } else status = STATUS_FSFILTER_OP_COMPLETED_SUCCESSFULLY;
  2134	        break;
  2135	    case IRP_MJ_RELEASE_FOR_SECTION_SYNCHRONIZATION:
  2136	        StageRelease(&stream->Resource);
  2137	        status = STATUS_FSFILTER_OP_COMPLETED_SUCCESSFULLY;
  2138	        break;
  2139	    case IRP_MJ_ACQUIRE_FOR_MOD_WRITE:
  2140	        KeEnterCriticalRegion();

  2145	        break;

  2147	        StageRelease(&stream->PagingResource);
  2148	        status = STATUS_FSFILTER_OP_COMPLETED_SUCCESSFULLY;
  2149	        break;
  2150	    case IRP_MJ_ACQUIRE_FOR_CC_FLUSH:
  2151	        StageAcquire(&stream->PagingResource);
  2152	        status = STATUS_FSFILTER_OP_COMPLETED_SUCCESSFULLY;
  2153	        break;
  2154	    case IRP_MJ_RELEASE_FOR_CC_FLUSH:
  2155	        StageRelease(&stream->PagingResource);
  2156	        status = STATUS_FSFILTER_OP_COMPLETED_SUCCESSFULLY;
  2157	        break;
  2158	    default:
  2159	        break; /* Never let a foreign filesystem decode an owned upper FCB. */
  2160	    }

  2165	            Data->Iopb->MajorFunction == IRP_MJ_SET_INFORMATION ?

  2171	NTSTATUS SafeUploadStageGenerateName(PFLT_INSTANCE Instance, PFILE_OBJECT FileObject,
  2172	    PFLT_CALLBACK_DATA Data, FLT_FILE_NAME_OPTIONS Options, PBOOLEAN CacheName,
  2173	    PFLT_NAME_CONTROL Output)
  2174	{
  2175	    PSTAGE_STREAM stream = StageStreamForObject(FileObject);
  2176	    PFLT_FILE_NAME_INFORMATION lower = NULL;
  2177	    NTSTATUS status;
  2178	    /* The bounded probe deliberately avoids a second namespace cache. Names
  2179	     * can change on every upper handle when a virtual rename completes. */
  2180	    *CacheName = FALSE;
  2181	    if (stream != NULL) {
  2182	        if (FltGetFileNameFormat(Options) == FLT_FILE_NAME_SHORT)
  2183	            return STATUS_OBJECT_NAME_NOT_FOUND;
  2184	        StageAcquire(&stream->Resource);
  2185	        status = FltCheckAndGrowNameControl(Output, stream->Name.Length);
  2186	        if (NT_SUCCESS(status)) RtlCopyUnicodeString(&Output->Name, &stream->Name);
  2187	        StageRelease(&stream->Resource);
  2188	        return status;
  2189	    }
  2190	    Options &= ~FLT_FILE_NAME_REQUEST_FROM_CURRENT_PROVIDER;
  2191	    Options |= FLT_FILE_NAME_DO_NOT_CACHE;
  2192	    if (Data != NULL) status = FltGetFileNameInformation(Data, Options, &lower);
  2193	    else status = FltGetFileNameInformationUnsafe(FileObject, Instance, Options, &lower);
  2194	    if (NT_SUCCESS(status)) {
  2195	        status = FltCheckAndGrowNameControl(Output, lower->Name.Length);
  2196	        if (NT_SUCCESS(status)) RtlCopyUnicodeString(&Output->Name, &lower->Name);
  2197	        FltReleaseFileNameInformation(lower);
  2198	    }
  2199	    return status;
  2200	}

  2202	NTSTATUS SafeUploadStageNormalizeComponent(PFLT_INSTANCE Instance, PCUNICODE_STRING Parent,
  2203	    USHORT VolumeNameLength, PCUNICODE_STRING Component, PFILE_NAMES_INFORMATION Output,
  2204	    ULONG Length, FLT_NORMALIZE_NAME_FLAGS Flags, PVOID *Context)
  2205	{
  2206	    OBJECT_ATTRIBUTES attributes;
  2207	    IO_STATUS_BLOCK io;
  2208	    HANDLE directory = NULL;
  2209	    PFILE_OBJECT object = NULL;
  2210	    UNICODE_STRING parent = *Parent, component = *Component;
  2211	    ULONG returned;
  2212	    NTSTATUS status;
  2213	    UNREFERENCED_PARAMETER(VolumeNameLength);
  2214	    UNREFERENCED_PARAMETER(Context);
  2215	    InitializeObjectAttributes(&attributes, &parent, OBJ_KERNEL_HANDLE |
  2216	        (FlagOn(Flags, FLTFL_NORMALIZE_NAME_CASE_SENSITIVE) ? 0 : OBJ_CASE_INSENSITIVE), NULL, NULL);
  2217	    status = FltCreateFileEx(SafeUploadData.Filter, Instance, &directory, &object,
  2218	        FILE_LIST_DIRECTORY | SYNCHRONIZE, &attributes, &io, NULL, FILE_ATTRIBUTE_NORMAL,
  2219	        FILE_SHARE_READ | FILE_SHARE_WRITE | FILE_SHARE_DELETE, FILE_OPEN,
  2220	        FILE_DIRECTORY_FILE | FILE_SYNCHRONOUS_IO_NONALERT, NULL, 0, IO_IGNORE_SHARE_ACCESS_CHECK);
  2221	    if (NT_SUCCESS(status)) {
  2222	        status = FltQueryDirectoryFile(Instance, object, Output, Length,
  2223	            FileNamesInformation, TRUE, &component, TRUE, &returned);
  2224	        ObDereferenceObject(object);
  2225	        FltClose(directory);
  2226	    }
  2227	    return status;
  2228	}

  2230	
  2231	/* Caller owns NamespaceResource, then Resource. Sections cannot be recreated
  2232	 * without an upper file object; the namespace lock excludes fresh CREATEs. */
  2233	static NTSTATUS StageDrain(PSTAGE_STREAM Stream)
  2234	{
  2235	    NTSTATUS status;
  2236	    if (Stream->ShareAccess.OpenCount != 0 || !MmCanFileBeTruncated(&Stream->Sections, NULL))
  2237	        return STATUS_DEVICE_BUSY;
  2238	    status = StageFlush(Stream);
  2239	    if (!NT_SUCCESS(status)) return status;
  2240	    if (!CcPurgeCacheSection(&Stream->Sections, NULL, 0, TRUE) ||
  2241	        Stream->Sections.SharedCacheMap != NULL || Stream->Sections.DataSectionObject != NULL ||
  2242	        Stream->Sections.ImageSectionObject != NULL ||
  2243	        InterlockedCompareExchange(&Stream->FileObjects, 0, 0) != 0) return STATUS_DEVICE_BUSY;
  2244	    /* Postoperations, including asynchronous paging and Cc AdvanceOnly, release
  2245	     * this rundown. It is forbidden to close the backing before they finish. */
  2246	    ExWaitForRundownProtectionRelease(&Stream->PagingRundown);
  2247	    status = Stream->ReadOnly ? STATUS_SUCCESS : FltFlushBuffers(Stream->BackingInstance, Stream->BackingObject);
  2248	    ExReInitializeRundownProtection(&Stream->PagingRundown);
  2249	    return status;
  2250	}
  2251	
  2252	static KSTART_ROUTINE StageWorker;
  2253	static VOID StageWorker(PVOID Context)
  2254	{
  2255	    LARGE_INTEGER interval;
  2256	    PLIST_ENTRY link;
  2257	    UNREFERENCED_PARAMETER(Context);
  2258	    interval.QuadPart = -250 * 10000LL;
  2259	    while (KeWaitForSingleObject(&StageWorkerStop, Executive, KernelMode, FALSE, &interval) == STATUS_TIMEOUT) {
  2260	        StageAcquire(&StageNamespaceResource);
  2261	        if (!StageStopping) for (link = StageStreams.Flink; link != &StageStreams; link = link->Flink) {
  2262	            PSTAGE_STREAM stream = CONTAINING_RECORD(link, STAGE_STREAM, Link);
  2263	            NTSTATUS status = STATUS_SUCCESS;
  2264	            if (stream->RenameExchange != NULL) StageFinishRename(stream);
  2265	            if (stream->Sealed || stream->RenameExchange != NULL) continue;
  2266	            StageAcquire(&stream->Resource);
  2267	            if (!stream->ReadOnly) {
  2268	                status = StageDrain(stream);
  2269	                if (NT_SUCCESS(status)) {
  2270	                    StageCloseBacking(stream);
  2271	                    stream->ReadOnly = TRUE; /* Irreversible, even if reopen/notification fails. */
  2272	                }
  2273	            }
  2274	            if (NT_SUCCESS(status) && stream->BackingObject == NULL) status = StageOpenBacking(stream, TRUE);
  2275	            StageRelease(&stream->Resource);
  2276	            if (NT_SUCCESS(status)) {
  2277	                status = SafeUploadStageSeal(stream->View->OwnerProcessId, &stream->StageName);
  2278	                if (NT_SUCCESS(status)) stream->Sealed = TRUE;
  2279	            }
  2280	        }
  2281	        StageRelease(&StageNamespaceResource);
  2282	    }
  2283	    PsTerminateSystemThread(STATUS_SUCCESS);
  2284	}
  2285	
  2286	/* Phase 3 admission no longer consumes StageFence's directory scan, scan-built SOP fence,
  2287	 * quarantine/retry table, or policy refresh mutex. The bounded writer registry and active SOP map
  2288	 * replace enumeration and scan-built paging/read denial; targeted file-ID probes replace broad
  2289	 * scans; attach trust plus per-instance/file Unknown replaces quarantine; policy epochs and the
  2290	 * service's durable two-phase commit replace scan/swap serialization; cleanup, section/transaction
  2291	 * completion, and targeted reclaim replace retry timers; registry/epoch pages replace fence
  2292	 * counters. StageFence remains buildable only for explicit diagnostic refresh/status and setup/
  2293	 * unload coordination; no boot-policy, SET_POLICY, or file-I/O admission path calls it. */
  2294	NTSTATUS SafeUploadStageInitialize(VOID)
  2295	{
  2296	    NTSTATUS status;
  2297	    InitializeListHead(&StageStreams);
  2298	    InitializeListHead(&StageViews);
  2299	    KeInitializeSpinLock(&StageListLock);
  2300	    KeInitializeEvent(&StageWorkerStop, NotificationEvent, FALSE);
  2301	    SafeUploadStageInitializeProtocol();
  2302	    status = ExInitializeResourceLite(&StageNamespaceResource);
  2303	    if (NT_SUCCESS(status)) {
  2304	#if SAFEUPLOAD_STAGING_PROTOTYPE
  2305	        SafeUploadStageWritersInitialize();
  2306	        status = SafeUploadStageFenceInitialize();
  2307	        if (!NT_SUCCESS(status)) return status;
  2308	        ExInitializeFastMutex(&AdmissionTraceControlMutex);
  2309	        ExInitializeRundownProtection(&AdmissionTraceRundown);
  2310	        AdmissionTraceRundownClosed = FALSE;
  2311	        InterlockedExchange(&SafeUploadAdmissionTraceControlState, 0);
  2312	        InterlockedExchange(&SafeUploadAdmissionTraceSectionEvents, 0);
  2313	        InterlockedExchange(&AdmissionTraceLifetimeEvents, 0);
  2314	        StageAdmissionTraceReset();
  2315	#endif
  2360	    _Out_ PUINT32 Reason, _Out_ PUINT32 Status)
  2361	{
  2362	    UINT64 veto = (UINT64)InterlockedCompareExchange64(&StageLastUnloadVeto, 0, 0);
  2363	    *Streams = StageStreamCount;
  2364	    *FileObjects = (UINT32)InterlockedCompareExchange(&StageFileObjects, 0, 0);
  2365	    *Reason = (UINT32)(veto >> 32);
  2366	    *Status = (UINT32)veto;
  2367	}
  2368	
  2369	NTSTATUS SafeUploadStagePrepareUnload(VOID)
  2370	{
  2371	    PLIST_ENTRY link;
  2372	    NTSTATUS status = STATUS_SUCCESS;
  2373	#if SAFEUPLOAD_STAGING_PROTOTYPE
  2374	    /* Rescan to drop stale entries, then refuse while a fenced mapped stream remains: unloading would
  2375	     * release its old view's writes. Before any namespace lock, so the scan never nests inside one. */
  2376	    SafeUploadStageRecordUnloadVeto(SAFEUPLOAD_UNLOAD_VETO_NONE, STATUS_SUCCESS);
  2377	    status = SafeUploadStageFencePrepareUnload();
  2378	    if (!NT_SUCCESS(status)) {
  2379	        SafeUploadStageRecordUnloadVeto(SAFEUPLOAD_UNLOAD_VETO_FENCE, status);
  2380	        return STATUS_FLT_DO_NOT_DETACH;
  2381	    }
  2382	#endif
  2383	    StageAcquire(&StageNamespaceResource);
  2384	    StageStopping = TRUE;
  2385	    for (link = StageStreams.Flink; link != &StageStreams; link = link->Flink) {
  2386	        PSTAGE_STREAM stream = CONTAINING_RECORD(link, STAGE_STREAM, Link);
  2387	        StageAcquire(&stream->Resource);
  2388	        status = StageDrain(stream);
  2389	        StageRelease(&stream->Resource);
  2390	        if (!NT_SUCCESS(status)) break;
  2391	    }
  2392	    if (!NT_SUCCESS(status) || InterlockedCompareExchange(&StageFileObjects, 0, 0) != 0) {
  2393	        SafeUploadStageRecordUnloadVeto(!NT_SUCCESS(status) ? SAFEUPLOAD_UNLOAD_VETO_STAGE_DRAIN :
  2394	            SAFEUPLOAD_UNLOAD_VETO_STAGE_OBJECTS, !NT_SUCCESS(status) ? status : STATUS_DEVICE_BUSY);
  2395	        StageStopping = FALSE;
  2396	        StageRelease(&StageNamespaceResource);
  2397	        return STATUS_FLT_DO_NOT_DETACH;
  2398	    }
  2399	    StageRelease(&StageNamespaceResource);
  2400	#if SAFEUPLOAD_STAGING_PROTOTYPE
  2401	    /* This is the unload's commit point: every fallible stage check has completed, and no setup,
  2402	     * scan, or quarantine may appear after it. A veto is still reversible because the worker and
  2403	     * backing objects have not yet been stopped or released. */
  2404	    if (!SafeUploadStageFenceTryCommitUnload()) {
  2405	        SafeUploadStageRecordUnloadVeto(SAFEUPLOAD_UNLOAD_VETO_FENCE_COMMIT, STATUS_DEVICE_BUSY);
  2406	        StageAcquire(&StageNamespaceResource);
  2407	        StageStopping = FALSE;
  2408	        StageRelease(&StageNamespaceResource);
  2409	        return STATUS_FLT_DO_NOT_DETACH;
  2410	    }
  2411	#endif
  2412	    SafeUploadStageStopWorker();
  2413	    /* Instance references must be dropped BEFORE FltUnregisterFilter. */
  2414	    for (link = StageStreams.Flink; link != &StageStreams; link = link->Flink) {
  2415	        PSTAGE_STREAM stream = CONTAINING_RECORD(link, STAGE_STREAM, Link);
  2416	        ExWaitForRundownProtectionRelease(&stream->PagingRundown);
  2417	        StageCloseBacking(stream);
  2418	        if (stream->BackingInstance != NULL) FltObjectDereference(stream->BackingInstance);
  2419	        stream->BackingInstance = NULL;
  2420	        if (stream->OriginalInstance != NULL) FltObjectDereference(stream->OriginalInstance);
  2421	        stream->OriginalInstance = NULL;
  2422	    }
  2423	    return STATUS_SUCCESS;
  2424	}
  2425	
  2426	VOID SafeUploadStageFree(VOID)
  2427	{
  2428	    if (!StageInitialized) return;
  2429	#if SAFEUPLOAD_STAGING_PROTOTYPE
  2430	    StageAdmissionTraceShutdown();
  2431	    SafeUploadStageFenceFree();
  2432	#endif
  2433	    while (!IsListEmpty(&StageStreams)) {
  2434	        PSTAGE_STREAM stream = CONTAINING_RECORD(RemoveHeadList(&StageStreams), STAGE_STREAM, Link);
  2435	        FsRtlTeardownPerStreamContexts(&stream->Header);
  2436	        StageFreeStream(stream);
  2437	    }
  2438	    while (!IsListEmpty(&StageViews)) StageFreeView(CONTAINING_RECORD(RemoveHeadList(&StageViews), STAGE_VIEW, Link));
  2439	#if SAFEUPLOAD_STAGING_PROTOTYPE
  2440	    SafeUploadStageWritersUninitialize();
  2441	#endif
  2442	    ExDeleteResourceLite(&StageNamespaceResource);
  2443	    StageInitialized = FALSE;
  2444	}
  2445	
  3250	    if (result != FLT_PREOP_SUCCESS_NO_CALLBACK) return result;
  3251	    /* Owned-stream routing MUST precede all legacy taint/context/policy callbacks. */
  3252	    if (StageStreamForObject(Data->Iopb->TargetFileObject) != NULL)
  3253	        return StagePreOperation(Data, Objects, CompletionContext, AdmissionToken);

  3261	        NTSTATUS reserveStatus;

  3266	        if (result == FLT_PREOP_COMPLETE) return result;
  3267	#if SAFEUPLOAD_STAGING_PROTOTYPE
  3268	        /* Reserve bounded registry capacity before a physical writer create reaches NTFS. A
  3269	         * tracking failure records Unknown and leaves this admission decision unchanged. */
  3270	        reserveStatus = SafeUploadStageWritersReserveCreate(Data, Objects,
  3271	            &writerReservation, &reservationRequired);
  3272	        if (!NT_SUCCESS(reserveStatus)) {
  3273	            if (writerReservation != NULL)
  3274	                SafeUploadStageWritersCancelReservation(writerReservation);
  3275	            SafeUploadStageWritersTrackingLost(Objects->Instance,
  3276	                SAFEUPLOAD_REGISTRY_UNKNOWN_ALLOCATION);
  3277	            reservationRequired = FALSE;
  3278	            writerReservation = NULL;
  3279	        }
  3280	        if (reservationRequired && writerReservation != NULL) {

  3282	                result == FLT_PREOP_SUCCESS_WITH_CALLBACK);
  3283	            *CompletionContext = writerReservation;

  3287	            SafeUploadStageWritersTrackingLost(Objects->Instance,

  3292	        }
  3293	    case IRP_MJ_QUERY_OPEN:

  3299	            if (FLT_IS_FASTIO_OPERATION(Data)) return FLT_PREOP_DISALLOW_FASTIO;
  3300	        }
  3301	        break;
  3302	    case IRP_MJ_DIRECTORY_CONTROL:

  3321	    case IRP_MJ_MDL_READ_COMPLETE:
  3322	        /* Completion releases a previously returned MDL; do not block the release path. */
  3323	        break;
  3324	    case IRP_MJ_PREPARE_MDL_WRITE:
  3325	        /* PREPARE_MDL_WRITE fast I/O bypasses W. A retry uses IRP_MJ_WRITE below, which counts
  3326	         * W through completion for IRP_MN_MDL and IRP_MN_COMPLETE_MDL too. */

  3329	            BOOLEAN trackedWriter = SafeUploadStageWritersIsTrackedWriter(
  3330	                Objects->Instance, Objects->FileObject);
  3331	            if (trackedWriter) return FLT_PREOP_DISALLOW_FASTIO;

  3333	                SafeUploadPolicyMayMatchInstanceVolume(Objects->Instance)) {

  3413	    case IRP_MJ_SET_INFORMATION:

  3593	        *CompletionContext = NULL;

  3597	        result == FLT_PREOP_SYNCHRONIZE) {

  3612	static FLT_POSTOP_CALLBACK_STATUS StagePostOperationCore(PFLT_CALLBACK_DATA Data,
  3613	    PCFLT_RELATED_OBJECTS Objects, PVOID CompletionContext, FLT_POST_OPERATION_FLAGS Flags)
  3614	{
  3615	#if SAFEUPLOAD_STAGING_PROTOTYPE
  3616	    if (Data->Iopb->MajorFunction == IRP_MJ_ACQUIRE_FOR_SECTION_SYNCHRONIZATION && CompletionContext != NULL) {
  3617	        if (FlagOn(Flags, FLTFL_POST_OPERATION_DRAINING)) {
  3618	            /* Outcome unavailable while draining: keep the entry and make writer state Unknown. */
  3619	            SafeUploadStageSectionAcquireDraining(Objects->Instance, CompletionContext);
  3620	        } else if (!NT_SUCCESS(Data->IoStatus.Status)) {
  3621	            /* The acquire failed below us: no release follows, so drop the in-flight entry here. */
  3622	            SafeUploadStageSectionAcquireFailed(CompletionContext);
  3623	        }
  3624	        return FLT_POSTOP_FINISHED_PROCESSING;
  3625	    }
  3626	    if (Data->Iopb->MajorFunction == IRP_MJ_SET_INFORMATION &&
  3627	        SafeUploadStageWritersIsRenameContext(CompletionContext)) {
  3628	        SafeUploadStageWritersSetMutatingIoCompletion(CompletionContext, Data, Flags);
  3629	        SafeUploadStageWritersCompleteRename(Objects->Instance, CompletionContext,
  3630	            !FlagOn(Flags, FLTFL_POST_OPERATION_DRAINING) && Data->IoStatus.Status == STATUS_SUCCESS,
  3631	            FlagOn(Flags, FLTFL_POST_OPERATION_DRAINING));
  3632	        return FLT_POSTOP_FINISHED_PROCESSING;
  3633	    }
  3634	    if ((Data->Iopb->MajorFunction == IRP_MJ_WRITE ||
  3635	         Data->Iopb->MajorFunction == IRP_MJ_SET_INFORMATION ||
  3636	         Data->Iopb->MajorFunction == IRP_MJ_SET_EA ||
  3637	         Data->Iopb->MajorFunction == IRP_MJ_SET_SECURITY ||
  3638	         Data->Iopb->MajorFunction == IRP_MJ_FILE_SYSTEM_CONTROL) &&
  3639	        SafeUploadStageWritersIsMutatingIoContext(CompletionContext)) {
  3640	        /* Includes ordinary success/failure and POST_OPERATION_DRAINING. */
  3641	        SafeUploadStageWritersSetMutatingIoCompletion(CompletionContext, Data, Flags);
  3642	        SafeUploadStageWritersEndMutatingIo(CompletionContext);
  3643	        return FLT_POSTOP_FINISHED_PROCESSING;
  3644	    }
  3645	    if (Data->Iopb->MajorFunction == IRP_MJ_RELEASE_FOR_SECTION_SYNCHRONIZATION) {
  3646	        SafeUploadStageSectionReleaseComplete(Objects->Instance, CompletionContext,
  3647	            !FlagOn(Flags, FLTFL_POST_OPERATION_DRAINING) && NT_SUCCESS(Data->IoStatus.Status),
  3648	            FlagOn(Flags, FLTFL_POST_OPERATION_DRAINING));
  3649	        return FLT_POSTOP_FINISHED_PROCESSING;
  3650	    }
  3651	    if (Data->Iopb->MajorFunction == IRP_MJ_CLEANUP) {
  3652	        if (FlagOn(Flags, FLTFL_POST_OPERATION_DRAINING)) {
  3653	            SafeUploadStageWritersMutationDraining(Objects->Instance,
  3654	                Objects->FileObject != NULL ? Objects->FileObject->SectionObjectPointer : NULL);
  3655	        } else if (NT_SUCCESS(Data->IoStatus.Status)) {
  3656	            SafeUploadStageWritersOnCleanup(Data, Objects);
  3657	            SafeUploadStageWritersQueueRecheck();
  3658	        }
  3659	    }
  3660	#endif
  3661	    if (Data->Iopb->MajorFunction == IRP_MJ_CREATE) {
  3662	#if SAFEUPLOAD_STAGING_PROTOTYPE
  3663	        if (SafeUploadStageWritersIsReservation(CompletionContext)) {
  3664	            PVOID legacyCompletionContext = NULL;
  3665	            BOOLEAN legacyCallbackRequired = FALSE;
  3666	            NTSTATUS writerStatus = SafeUploadStageWritersPostCreate(Data, Objects, Flags,
  3667	                CompletionContext, &legacyCompletionContext, &legacyCallbackRequired);
  3668	            if (!NT_SUCCESS(writerStatus)) return FLT_POSTOP_FINISHED_PROCESSING;
  3669	            if (legacyCallbackRequired)
  3670	                return SafeUploadPostCreate(Data, Objects, legacyCompletionContext, Flags);
  3671	            return FLT_POSTOP_FINISHED_PROCESSING;
  3672	        }
  3673	#endif
  3674	        return SafeUploadPostCreate(Data, Objects, CompletionContext, Flags);
  3675	    }

  1221	static NTSTATUS StageSetIdentity(PSTAGE_VIEW View, PCWSTR Basename)
  1222	{
  1223	    ULONG index;
  1224	    PLIST_ENTRY link;
  1225	    for (index = 0; index < sizeof(View->Identity.FileId.Identifier); ++index) {

  1228	            WCHAR ch = Basename[index * 2 + nibble];
  1229	            ULONG value = ch >= L'0' && ch <= L'9' ? ch - L'0' :
  1230	                ch >= L'a' && ch <= L'f' ? ch - L'a' + 10 :
  1231	                ch >= L'A' && ch <= L'F' ? ch - L'A' + 10 : 16;
  1232	            if (value > 15) return STATUS_INVALID_PARAMETER;
  1233	            byte = byte * 16 + value;
  1234	        }
  1235	        View->Identity.FileId.Identifier[index] = (UCHAR)byte;
  1236	    }
  1237	    /* Distinguish private IDs from NTFS's physical 64-bit reference numbers. */
  1238	    View->Identity.FileId.Identifier[15] |= 0x80;
  1239	    View->Identity.VolumeSerialNumber = View->ParentIdentity.VolumeSerialNumber;
  1240	    for (link = StageViews.Flink; link != &StageViews; link = link->Flink) {
  1241	        PSTAGE_VIEW other = CONTAINING_RECORD(link, STAGE_VIEW, Link);
  1242	        if (other->Identity.VolumeSerialNumber == View->Identity.VolumeSerialNumber &&
  1243	            RtlEqualMemory(&other->Identity.FileId, &View->Identity.FileId, sizeof(FILE_ID_128)))
  1244	            return STATUS_OBJECT_NAME_COLLISION;
  1245	    }
  1246	    return STATUS_SUCCESS;
  1247	}
  1248	
  1249	static PSTAGE_STREAM StageHiddenStream(PEPROCESS Owner, PUNICODE_STRING Name)
  1250	{
  1251	    PLIST_ENTRY link, old;
  1252	    PSTAGE_STREAM found = NULL;
  1253	    LONG64 newest = 0;
  1254	    for (link = StageViews.Flink; link != &StageViews; link = link->Flink) {
  1255	        PSTAGE_VIEW view = CONTAINING_RECORD(link, STAGE_VIEW, Link);
  1256	        if (view->Owner != Owner) continue;
  1257	        for (old = view->OldNames.Flink; old != &view->OldNames; old = old->Flink) {
  1258	            PSTAGE_OLD_NAME tombstone = CONTAINING_RECORD(old, STAGE_OLD_NAME, Link);
  1259	            if (tombstone->Sequence > newest && RtlEqualUnicodeString(&tombstone->Name, Name, TRUE)) {
  1260	                newest = tombstone->Sequence;
  1261	                found = tombstone->Version;
  1262	            }
  1263	        }
  1264	    }
  1265	    return found;
  1266	}
  1267	
  1268	static BOOLEAN StageHiddenName(PEPROCESS Owner, PUNICODE_STRING Name)
  1269	{
  1270	    return StageHiddenStream(Owner, Name) != NULL;
  1271	}
  1272	
  1273	static VOID StageFreeView(PSTAGE_VIEW View)
  1274	{
  1275	    while (!IsListEmpty(&View->OldNames)) ExFreePoolWithTag(CONTAINING_RECORD(
  1276	        RemoveHeadList(&View->OldNames), STAGE_OLD_NAME, Link), STAGE_TAG);
  1277	    if (View->Owner != NULL) ObDereferenceObject(View->Owner);
  1278	    SafeUploadStageFreeSecurity(View->Security, View->AssignedSecurity);
  1279	    ExFreePoolWithTag(View, STAGE_TAG);
  1280	}
  1281	
  1282	/* NamespaceResource serializes CREATE, rotation, retarget and retirement.
  1283	 * An upper FILE_OBJECT references a version, never a mutable current pointer. */
  1284	static NTSTATUS StageCreate(PFLT_CALLBACK_DATA Data, PCFLT_RELATED_OBJECTS Objects,
  1285	    PFLT_FILE_NAME_INFORMATION Name, SAFEUPLOAD_VOLUME_KIND Kind, BOOLEAN Writer,
  1286	    PSTAGE_VIEW ExpectedView, BOOLEAN PrivateNamespace, PBOOLEAN Handled)
  1287	{
  1288	    PSTAGE_VIEW view = NULL;
  1289	    PSTAGE_STREAM hiddenStream = NULL;
  1290	    PSTAGE_STREAM stream = NULL;
  1291	    PSTAGE_HANDLE handle = NULL;
  1292	    PFILE_OBJECT file = Data->Iopb->TargetFileObject;
  1293	    ACCESS_MASK desired = Data->Iopb->Parameters.Create.SecurityContext->DesiredAccess;
  1294	    ACCESS_MASK granted = 0;
  1295	    ULONG disposition = Data->Iopb->Parameters.Create.Options >> 24;
  1296	    ULONG information = FILE_OPENED;
  1297	    PEPROCESS owner = FltGetRequestorProcess(Data);
  1298	    BOOLEAN newView = FALSE, newStream = FALSE, exists = FALSE;
  1299	    FLT_FILESYSTEM_TYPE fs;
  1300	    WCHAR basename[SAFEUPLOAD_MAX_STAGE_NAME_CHARS];
  1301	    USHORT basenameLength;
  1302	    KIRQL irql;
  1303	    NTSTATUS status = STATUS_SUCCESS;
  1304	    *Handled = TRUE;
  1305	    if (owner == NULL) return STATUS_ACCESS_DENIED;
  1306	    StageAcquire(&StageNamespaceResource);
  1307	    if (StageStopping) { status = STATUS_DEVICE_NOT_READY; goto Exit; }
  1308	    view = StageFindView(owner, &Name->Name);
  1309	    /* A name snapshot for a file-ID open must still name that same live view.
  1310	     * Rename/replacement between lookup and admission must never create a new
  1311	     * view or return the newly occupying file. Views remain allocated to unload. */
  1312	    if (ExpectedView != NULL && (view != ExpectedView ||
  1313	        view->Current->OriginalInstance != Objects->Instance)) {
  1314	        status = STATUS_SHARING_VIOLATION; goto Exit;
  1315	    }
  1316	    if (view == NULL) hiddenStream = StageHiddenStream(owner, &Name->Name);
  1317	    if (hiddenStream != NULL) {
  1318	        if (!Writer || disposition == FILE_OPEN || disposition == FILE_OVERWRITE) {
  1319	            status = STATUS_OBJECT_NAME_NOT_FOUND; goto Exit;
  1320	        }
  1321	        /* Logical absence is distinct from an occupied public name. The
  1322	         * service must consume this writer's CURRENT committed tombstone. */
  1323	    }
  1324	    if (view == NULL && !Writer) {
  1325	        if (PrivateNamespace) status = STATUS_OBJECT_NAME_NOT_FOUND;
  1326	        else *Handled = FALSE;
  1327	        goto Exit;
  1328	    }
  1329	    if (Name->Name.Length > sizeof(stream->NameBuffer) || Name->Stream.Length != 0 ||
  1330	        (FlagOn(Data->Iopb->Parameters.Create.Options, FILE_OPEN_BY_FILE_ID) && ExpectedView == NULL) ||
  1331	        FlagOn(Data->Iopb->Parameters.Create.Options, FILE_DIRECTORY_FILE |
  1332	            FILE_DELETE_ON_CLOSE | FILE_NO_INTERMEDIATE_BUFFERING) ||
  1333	        file->FsContext != NULL || file->FsContext2 != NULL) {
  1334	        status = STATUS_NOT_SUPPORTED; goto Exit;
  1335	    }
  1336	    status = FltGetFileSystemType(Objects->Instance, &fs);
  1337	    if (!NT_SUCCESS(status)) goto Exit;
  1338	    if (fs != FLT_FSTYPE_NTFS || Kind == SafeUploadVolumeNetwork) {
  1339	        status = STATUS_NOT_SUPPORTED; goto Exit;
  1340	    }
  1341	    if (view == NULL) {
  1342	        view = ExAllocatePool2(POOL_FLAG_NON_PAGED, sizeof(*view), STAGE_TAG);
  1343	        if (view == NULL) { status = STATUS_INSUFFICIENT_RESOURCES; goto Exit; }
  1344	        newView = TRUE;
  1345	        InitializeListHead(&view->OldNames);
  1346	        status = SafeUploadStageCaptureSecurity(Data, Objects->Instance, &Name->Name, hiddenStream != NULL,
  1347	            &view->Security, &view->AssignedSecurity, &exists, &granted);
  1348	        if (!NT_SUCCESS(status)) goto Exit;
  1349	        {
  1350	            UNICODE_STRING parent = Name->Name;
  1351	            USHORT index;
  1352	            for (index = parent.Length / sizeof(WCHAR); index > 0; --index)
  1353	                if (parent.Buffer[index - 1] == L'\\') break;
  1354	            if (index == 0) { status = STATUS_OBJECT_PATH_INVALID; goto Exit; }
  1355	            parent.Length = (index - 1) * sizeof(WCHAR);
  1356	            parent.MaximumLength = parent.Length;
  1357	            status = SafeUploadStageQueryIdentity(Objects->Instance, &parent, TRUE, &view->ParentIdentity);
  1358	            if (!NT_SUCCESS(status)) goto Exit;
  1359	            view->PhysicalExists = exists;
  1360	            if (exists || hiddenStream != NULL) {
  1361	                status = SafeUploadStageQueryIdentity(Objects->Instance, &Name->Name, FALSE, &view->PhysicalIdentity);
  1362	                if (!exists && status == STATUS_OBJECT_NAME_NOT_FOUND) status = STATUS_SUCCESS;
  1363	                else if (!NT_SUCCESS(status)) goto Exit;
  1364	                else view->PhysicalExists = TRUE;
  1365	                if (view->PhysicalExists && view->PhysicalIdentity.VolumeSerialNumber != view->ParentIdentity.VolumeSerialNumber) {
  1366	                    status = STATUS_NOT_SAME_DEVICE; goto Exit;
  1367	                }
  1368	            }
  1369	        }
  1370	        view->Owner = owner; ObReferenceObject(owner);
  1371	        view->OwnerProcessId = FltGetRequestorProcessId(Data);
  1372	        view->Name.Buffer = view->NameBuffer;
  1373	        view->Name.MaximumLength = sizeof(view->NameBuffer);
  1374	        RtlCopyUnicodeString(&view->Name, &Name->Name);
  1375	    } else {
  1376	        exists = TRUE;
  1377	        if (disposition == FILE_CREATE) { status = STATUS_OBJECT_NAME_COLLISION; goto Exit; }
  1378	        status = SafeUploadStageCheckAccess(Data, view->Security, desired, &granted);
  1379	        if (!NT_SUCCESS(status)) goto Exit;
  1380	        stream = view->Current;
  1381	        if (stream->RenameExchange != NULL || (stream->ReadOnly && !stream->Sealed)) {
  1382	            status = STATUS_SHARING_VIOLATION; goto Exit;
  1383	        }
  1384	    }
  1385	    if (stream == NULL || (Writer && stream->Sealed)) {
  1386	        PSTAGE_STREAM previous = stream;
  1387	        if (StageStreamCount == STAGE_LIMIT) { status = STATUS_INSUFFICIENT_RESOURCES; goto Exit; }
  1388	        stream = ExAllocatePool2(POOL_FLAG_NON_PAGED, sizeof(*stream), STAGE_TAG);
  1389	        if (stream == NULL) { status = STATUS_INSUFFICIENT_RESOURCES; goto Exit; }

  1393	        if (stream->ByteLocks == NULL) { status = STATUS_INSUFFICIENT_RESOURCES; goto Exit; }

  1412	        stream->Name.Buffer = stream->NameBuffer;
  1413	        stream->Name.MaximumLength = sizeof(stream->NameBuffer);
  1414	        RtlCopyUnicodeString(&stream->Name, &Name->Name);

  1417	            hiddenStream != NULL ? &hiddenStream->StageName : NULL, basename, &basenameLength);

  1420	            status = StageSetIdentity(view, basename);
  1421	            if (!NT_SUCCESS(status)) goto Exit;
  1422	        }

  1424	            L"\\??\\C:\\ProgramData\\SafeUpload\\staging\\%ws", basename);

  1427	        status = StageOpenBacking(stream, FALSE);
  1428	        if (!NT_SUCCESS(status)) goto Exit;
  1429	    }
  1430	    if (FLT_IS_IRP_OPERATION(Data) && FltIsIoCanceled(Data)) {
  1431	        status = STATUS_CANCELLED; goto Exit;
  1432	    }
  1433	    handle = ExAllocatePool2(POOL_FLAG_NON_PAGED, sizeof(*handle), STAGE_TAG);
  1434	    if (handle == NULL) { status = STATUS_INSUFFICIENT_RESOURCES; goto Exit; }

236-    - [ ] Verify and qualify transactional policy acceptance: the feature
237-          path now keeps the previous policy current and the candidate pending
238-          through two checked old-and-candidate union scans before publication;
239-          scan failure leaves the old policy current and the last successfully
240-          installed fence table intact. This removes the unchecked post-swap refresh but
241:          does not close the mapping race. A proposed pre/post-operation rundown
242-          was removed: the minifilter post-op completes before Memory Manager
243:          creates the section. The later release callback carries no parameters
244-          and may not be observed if instance teardown begins, so a correctly
245-          paired, teardown-safe lease tracker is unresolved. A proposed provisional
246-          registry also lacks a retirement proof: `MmDoesFileHaveUserWritableReferences`
247:          reports currently user-mapped sections, while `CcPurgeCacheSection`
248:          purges system cache data and explicitly does not purge mapped files.
249:          An application can retain a writable file-mapping section handle
250-          without a mapped view, then map a writable view after policy commit;
251:          the documented section-create callback does not establish a later
252-          view-map barrier. `SECTION_OBJECT_POINTERS.DataSectionObject` is not
253-          a documented retirement signal: filter drivers must treat its
254-          members as opaque, and the value can change at any time. See Microsoft's
255-          [MmDoesFileHaveUserWritableReferences contract](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows-hardware/drivers/ddi/ntifs/nf-ntifs-mmdoesfilehaveuserwritablereferences),
256:          [CcPurgeCacheSection contract](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows-hardware/drivers/ddi/ntifs/nf-ntifs-ccpurgecachesection),
257:          [SECTION_OBJECT_POINTERS contract](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows-hardware/drivers/ddi/wdm/ns-wdm-_section_object_pointers),
258:          and [section-object/view lifecycle](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows-hardware/drivers/kernel/managing-memory-sections).
259-          Any future registry must be visible to the paging-write gate before
260-          an acquire proceeds, preserve successful records until a documented
261-          proof rules out later writable views, and fail closed on capacity
262-          exhaustion or unmatched teardown. Sticky records avoid premature
263-          retirement but accumulate denials, and cannot protect writes after
--
266-          Luna separately assessed a sticky `FLT_STREAM_CONTEXT` marker as a
267-          bounded implementation candidate: install it on every stream in a
268-          qualified protected scope and check it in paging-write pre-operation
269-          before any owned-stream early return. This is not implemented or
270-          qualified. Context support is filesystem-specific, and the
271:          documentation does not promise that a live file-backed section keeps
272-          its stream context attached after user handles close. A namespace
273-          scan also races create, link, rename, and paging writes; an
274:          unenumerated stream with a retained section handle is a counterexample
275-          unless a volume-wide fallback covers it. Any trial therefore needs
276-          gated scope changes, drained paging writes during installation,
277-          fail-closed handling for missing/unsupported contexts and incomplete
278-          enumeration, plus target-stack lifetime tests. See Microsoft's
279-          [FltSetStreamContext contract](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows-hardware/drivers/ddi/fltkernel/nf-fltkernel-fltsetstreamcontext),
280:          [context cleanup](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows-hardware/drivers/ifs/managing-contexts),
281-          [stream-context support](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows-hardware/drivers/ddi/fltkernel/nf-fltkernel-fltsupportsstreamcontexts),
282-          and [pre-operation IRQL contract](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows-hardware/drivers/ifs/writing-preoperation-callback-routines).
283-          Keep this checkbox and DoD open pending a safe design, independent
284-          review, build and target-stack/runtime qualification. The current
285-          `FenceTryRelease` path also calls `FsRtlAcquireFileExclusive`, which
286-          Microsoft reserves for system use; `CcPurgeCacheSection` requires
287-          exclusive file ownership, so this is not a supported retirement
288-          proof. See the [reserved DDI notice](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows-hardware/drivers/ddi/ntifs/nf-ntifs-_fsrtl_advanced_fcb_header-fsrtlacquirefileexclusive)
289:          and [CcPurgeCacheSection requirements](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows-hardware/drivers/ddi/ntifs/nf-ntifs-ccpurgecachesection).

397-
398-This is a confirmed physical-destination leak on the latest qualified alias
399-feature SYS, `ACED8226913E062E5D3EE6FD0CF96963C3FD2C1FB2242EDBEDBB00768CCD44F8`.
400-On the isolated Windows 10 debuggee, the harness creates one GUID-named synthetic
401-file under the existing protected test prefix, opens it, and creates a writable
402:memory-mapped section while SafeUpload is unloaded. It closes the original file
403:handle while retaining the section and writable view, then loads that exact
404:feature SYS. A write and flush through the old view changes the bytes; a newly
405-opened file object reads `MAPPED AFTER FILTER ATTACH <GUID>` from the protected
406-destination. No agent/service or publication permit runs. The same run restores
407-the original driver and checks policy and Verifier; an independent following SSH
408-check confirms the original driver hash, filter unloaded, Manual/Stopped service,
409:original policy, Verifier off, zero agent processes/test tasks and removed
410:fixture. The earlier [open-handle run](evidence/2026-10-02/physical-mapping-gate.txt)
411-is retained as the initial reproduction; the closed-handle run is the stronger
412-current result.

447:The [closed-handle gate output](evidence/2026-10-02/physical-mapping-handle-closed-gate.txt)
448-records the VM UUID, driver/service/policy hashes, closed source handle,
449:retained writable section and fresh-file-object bytes. Its scope is one local
450-NTFS file on Windows 10 19045.2965 and this specific attach ordering; it is a
451-counterexample, not a complete concurrent admission matrix. The first
452-encoded-command launcher exceeded Windows' command-line limit. Two subsequent
453-harness attempts exposed PowerShell/.NET `MemoryMappedFile` overload binding
454:errors ([first](evidence/2026-10-02/physical-mapping-initial-failure.txt),
455:[second](evidence/2026-10-02/physical-mapping-second-failure.txt)); each stopped
456-before driver installation, and each `finally` block verified baseline and
457:removed its fixture. The final version uses a GUID-scoped mapping name and closes
458-the source file handle before filter attachment. Independent final state for
459-this run is in
460:[physical-mapping-handle-closed-final-restored-state.txt](evidence/2026-10-02/physical-mapping-handle-closed-final-restored-state.txt).
461-
462-This invalidates any claim that callback admission alone isolates all writes
463:after filter load. An existing writable section has no newly admitted protected
464:stream object for the feature path to seal or redirect, even after its source
465-file handle closes. Before production can activate staging on an attached
466-volume, the design must establish a race-safe admission epoch covering
467:outstanding file objects/sections and concurrent new opens, or safely refuse
468-protection/activation until that boundary is met. Prove the barrier against
469-mapped writes after handle close, attachment races and policy changes before
470-changing the architecture. Taint and disabled normal staging remain in force.
471-
472-The documented callback contracts constrain candidate fixes. Filter Manager's
473-[`InstanceSetupCallback`](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows-hardware/drivers/ddi/fltkernel/nc-fltkernel-pflt_instance_setup_callback)
474-distinguishes automatic attachment to existing volumes, newly mounted volumes,
475-manual attachment and detached volumes; it runs at PASSIVE_LEVEL and must not
476-perform thread synchronization or interprocess communication. A
477:[`SyncTypeCreateSection` callback](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows-hardware/drivers/ifs/flt-parameters-for-irp-mj-acquire-for-section-synchronization)
478:cannot be pended as a policy wait. Its section-specific page says
479-`SyncTypeOther` cannot fail and documents `STATUS_INSUFFICIENT_RESOURCES` when
480-memory is insufficient; it does not state that as the only possible failure.
481-The generic [minifilter completion](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows-hardware/drivers/ddi/fltkernel/nc-fltkernel-pflt_pre_operation_callback)
482-and [FS_FILTER callback](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows-hardware/drivers/ddi/ntifs/ns-ntifs-fs_filter_callbacks)
483-contracts permit an accurate non-success status, so `STATUS_ACCESS_DENIED` for
484-a policy-denied `SyncTypeCreateSection` appears contract-consistent, though the
485:section-specific page does not name that status. Validate it on each target
486-OS/filesystem stack; never fake memory exhaustion. The hard blocker is that
487-these callbacks do not drain writable views created before a gate: user code
488-can still store through a live view and modified pages can be written lazily
489-after handles close. This mapped-view lifetime gap remains a cutover blocker.
490-The documented
491-[`FltGetFileNameInformationUnsafe` constraints](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows-hardware/drivers/ddi/fltkernel/nf-fltkernel-fltgetfilenameinformationunsafe)
492-warn that filesystem name queries are unsafe in paging I/O and acquire/release
493:modified-page-writer callbacks; cache-only lookup avoids that query but can miss.
494-These contracts do not yet identify a qualified writeback callback solution.
495:No change to attachment or section admission is claimed by this evidence.
496-

513-which pushes the baseline policy (accepted by agent and driver; fixture still out
514-of scope); stop it; extend the policy file (`80694A0B…`) with the fixture directory;
515-start the real agent again, which pushes the expanded policy (accepted). A write
516:and flush through the old view then changes the file, and a fresh file object
517-reads `MAPPED AFTER POLICY CHANGE <GUID>`. No approval permit ran, so the bytes
518-are unapproved: `UnauthenticatedMappedWriteAfterPolicyExpansion=REPRODUCED`.

522:original policy, zero agent processes/tasks and no fixture or VHDX.
523:Checkpoint overlay: `/var/lib/libvirt/images/win10-debug.safeupload-pre-policy-transition-mapping-eof-20261002`.

525-above after the run; no executable line changed.)
526-
527-**Not claimed.** This is one NTFS file, one volume, Windows 10 19045.2965 and
528:one ordering with no concurrent race. The section was created **before any policy
529-existed**, not after the baseline policy was live: creating it with the agent and
530-baseline policy active was attempted and ended in the excluded access-denied runs
531:below, so "section created under an active policy, then scope expanded" is not
532-demonstrated. The run also cannot separate "predates the first policy" from
533-"predates the scope expansion"; the closing design must block both. It does not
534-show that an approved public overwrite or the agent's own publication is affected,

536-does not measure latency.

555:  capacity over a short file ([note](evidence/2026-10-02/policy-transition-mapping-before-policy-access-denied.txt),
556:  [raw](evidence/2026-10-02/policy-transition-mapping-before-policy-access-denied-raw.txt),

559:  zero errors) and the per-attempt `*-baseline.txt` files are preparation records.
560-
561-**Open question.** The access-denied results ended only in the run that
562:pre-sized the fixture to the mapping capacity *and* created the section before any
563:policy was pushed; two variables changed together. That is consistent with "section
564-creation needed an implicit file extension" but the denying component (driver or
565-NTFS) was never identified. Do not treat it as explained. If the feature driver is
566:refusing the extension or the section while an agent/policy is active, that is
567:separate behavior and also determines whether "section under active policy" can
568-be reproduced; it needs its own gate.
569-
570-The volume admission design must turn both this and the pre-attachment case into
571-blocked results with the same repro scripts, not merely one of them.
572-
--
580-implemented and both leaks remain open.
581-
582-- **Observer gap.** Both repro observers read the destination with a default
583-  buffered `FileStream` ([policy-transition](scripts/Test-StagedPolicyTransitionMapping.ps1),
584-  [pre-attachment](scripts/Test-StagedPreAttachmentMapping.ps1)). A buffered read
585:  goes through the file's shared cache, so a dirty mapped page is visible even if
586-  every paging write to disk were refused. Option 3's expected "fresh file
587-  object reads baseline bytes" therefore does not follow from its fence. It is
588:  also **unverified whether the recorded REPRODUCED results reflect the cache, the
589:  disk or both**. A blocked result needs both a cached reader and an uncached
590-  (no-buffering, write-through) reader of the physical bytes.
591-- **Blast radius.** The fence denies every unowned paging write whose file
592-  object has no current tag. Paging I/O cannot resolve scope by name, so
593-  pre-attachment objects of any process or file on the volume are refused:
594-  stuck dirty pages and possible system-file/registry effects. The design calls
595-  this "disruptive"; it is not acceptable as a production barrier.
596-- **Missed API, now verified.** [`MmDoesFileHaveUserWritableReferences`](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows-hardware/drivers/ddi/ntifs/nf-ntifs-mmdoesfilehaveuserwritablereferences)
597-  (ntifs.h, Vista and later, IRQL <= APC_LEVEL, takes `PSECTION_OBJECT_POINTERS`,
598:  returns 1 when the file has user-mapped sections) is documented, and states it
599-  "can be used to detect if there are writable views for a file object even when
600:  all file handles and section handles for the file object have been closed".
601-  This is the closed-handle case of both leaks. Documented in a transactional
602:  file-system context; **unverified here**: whether read-only mappings also return
603-  1, and whether an attribute-only physical open on NTFS exposes the same
604:  section pointers as the mapping's file object. [`MmCanFileBeTruncated`](https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/windows-hardware/drivers/ddi/ntifs/nf-ntifs-mmcanfilebetruncated)
605-  (< DISPATCH_LEVEL) is already used on owned streams (StageStream.c:615,1296).
606-
607-**Decision (direction, not yet a design).** Take option 1 as the base: per-stream
608-admission records and a documented per-file proof, refusing the protected open
609:(fail closed) when user-mapped sections exist, instead of a blanket paging-write
610:fence. Distinguish mount-time attach (no pre-attachment sections can exist for a
611-newly mounted volume; FltMgr reports this in the InstanceSetup flags, which are
612-cheap to read without synchronization) from late/manual attach, where staging
613-stays unavailable for that volume until its in-scope files are proven clean.
614-Any write fence must be scoped to identified protected streams, never volume-wide.
615-The recommended minimal slice is an **observe-only diagnostic** (no denials) to
616-settle the unknowns below on the VM before any enforcement code. Next worker
617:task: design v2 with exact data structures, call sites, lock order and test plan.
618-
619-Unknowns to settle empirically before enforcement: (1) does a pre-attachment
620:section's writeback reach this instance's paging `IRP_MJ_WRITE`, with which file
621:object and section pointers; (2) does `MmDoesFileHaveUserWritableReferences`
622-return 1 for writable and for read-only views while all handles are closed, using
623:section pointers from a READ_ATTRIBUTES physical open; (3) are those section
624:pointers identical to the mapping's file object; (4) what an uncached reader sees
625:after the old view writes and flushes, with and without a paging-write refusal.
626-
627-### Decision: design v2 accepted as the plan of record (2 October 2026)
628-
629-[admission-design-v2.txt](evidence/2026-10-02/admission-design-v2.txt) (brief:
630-[admission-design-v2-brief.md](evidence/2026-10-02/worker-briefs/admission-design-v2-brief.md)).
631-Core: one admission record per NTFS stream keyed by (instance, `SectionObjectPointer`),
632-anchored on `FLT_STREAM_CONTEXT` (not the stream-handle context, which dies when the
633-source handle closes in both repros); a documented per-file proof with
634-`MmDoesFileHaveUserWritableReferences`; a fence only for identified protected
635-streams; policy updates fail with `STATUS_DEVICE_BUSY` instead of waiting, with a
636:documented lock order that never holds `SafeUploadPolicyLock` over file or IPC work.
637-Existing `Flags` in InstanceSetup are currently ignored and need to be classified.
638-
639-Limits the worker found and I accept as the stated scope:
640-- **Leak (a) is not closed by per-file proofs.** A pre-attachment view can write
641-  before any protected open or policy scan identifies its stream, and paging I/O
642-  cannot resolve a name. Until a tested attach boundary exists, late/manual attach
643-  stays *Quarantined* and staging is **unavailable** for that volume (taint stays).
644:  "Newly mounted volume has no earlier sections" is a hypothesis to falsify
645-  (mount while loaded, remount, later stack attach), not a premise. Whether
646-  unavailability on late attach is acceptable for the product, and what the
647-  agent should do in that state, is a **cutover decision for the user**.
648-- 17 self-attacks: one OPEN (above), 16 need experiments; none validated.
649-- No enforcement is justified yet. Nine external contracts remain UNVERIFIED
650:  (see the v2 list), including section-pointer identity across opens, whether a
651:  pre-attachment writeback reaches this instance, and read-only mapping behavior.
652-
653-**Plan.** Slice 1 is an observe-only diagnostic: feature build only, **default off**
654-(enabled by a control message), no denials ever, a bounded 256-entry nonpaged ring
655-read through the existing filter port and `SafeUpload.Inspector`, plus new
656:repro variants that read with both a buffered and an uncached/write-through
657-reader. Run both orderings and the closed-handle writable/read-only probe to
658-answer unknowns 1-4 above. Only then design enforcement slices. Each kernel change gets a
659-fresh adversarial reviewer worker, four builds and the 275 agent tests, and a
660-disk-checkpointed VM run with an independent restoration check. A diagnostic result
661-is recorded as an observation, not as a blocked leak.
--
667-above before any enforcement code is written.
668-
669-What exists (feature build only, `SafeUploadStagingPrototype=true`; default OFF):
670-- A 256-entry nonpaged ring plus counters, enabled and read through the existing filter port.
671-  Hooks record unowned paging/nonpaging writes (target file object, `SectionObjectPointer`
672:  value, IRP flags, IRQL, PID), unowned acquire/release-for-section-synchronization, and
673-  InstanceSetup flags. Hooks do no context lookup, take no blocking lock and never change a
674-  status or completion path. `AdmissionRecordState` is the constant `not_tracked`: slice 1 has no
675-  admission-record table (revised slice contract).
676-- An **explicit probe** control (`SafeUpload.Inspector --admission-probe X:\dir\file`). It runs
677-  entirely in the Inspector's own message thread at PASSIVE_LEVEL: resolves this filter's

767-The four unknowns, as observed on Windows 10 19045.2965, NTFS, FltMgr 10.0.19041.1, one local volume, one fixture type:
768:1. **A pre-attachment writable mapping's writeback reaches this instance's `IRP_MJ_WRITE` hook: YES.** Unowned paging writes
769:   (`irpFlags 0x00060043`) carry the old view's section pointer (v1c seq 12, v3 seq 209 and 353, v4 seq 195).
770:2. **`MmDoesFileHaveUserWritableReferences` through an attribute-only open made after attachment:** writable mapping with the handle
771:   closed **yes**; writable mapping with the handle still open **yes**; **read-only mapping, handle closed: no**; no mapping: no (v3).
772-   The same `yes` under a live expanded policy (v4) and after a protected open (v2). The API therefore detects writable views only.
773-3. **Section-pointer identity: the probe's `SectionObjectPointer` equals the one on the old view's paging write, from a different
774:   `FILE_OBJECT`** (v1c, v3 A and D, v4; `sameFileObjectAsProbe=False` in every match), so a per-stream registry keyed by the section
775-   pointer can identify the writing stream with no name lookup. B and C (no writable view) had no matching write.
776:4. **Cached versus uncached: all three observers saw the written bytes** (buffered reader and uncached write-through reader opened
777:   before attachment, and a fresh buffered reader) in v1c, v2 and v4, so the change is visible to an uncached reader, not only
778:   through the cache. The behavior when a paging write is refused is **not** observed.
779-
780-Further observations, all baseline behavior of the unmodified admission logic:
781:- **v2:** with the real agent and a policy covering the file, a protected open of the file **succeeds while the old writable mapping
782:  exists**, and the old view's write still changes the bytes at all three readers. Admission does not consult mapping state today.
783-- The guest issues about 25 to 30 unowned paging writes per second (v3: 109 in the pre-write window), so any paging fence must be per
784-  stream, never volume-wide, which supports the design-v2 decision.
785-- v4 reproduces leak (b) under the diagnostic driver with the real agent: expanded policy accepted, then the old view's write is
786-  visible to all three readers.
